     1→# Building P2P Software Like Groove in Go
     2→
     3→Great question, Rob! Given your background in open-source hardware and distributed systems (like Safecast's monitoring network), you'll find Go is an excellent choice for P2P software. Groove (Microsoft's P2P collaboration platform from the early 2000s) had several key features worth understanding before designing your own.
     4→
     5→## What Made Groove Distinctive
     6→
     7→Groove was built around decentralized collaboration: shared workspaces, real-time sync, offline capability, presence awareness, and end-to-end encryption — all without requiring a central server for core functionality. Think of it as a peer-to-peer groupware system.
     8→
     9→## Architecture Overview in Go
    10→
    11→Here's how you'd approach this in Go, broken into layers:
    12→
    13→### 1. Networking & Peer Discovery
    14→
    15→The foundation is establishing connections between peers. **libp2p** (from Protocol Labs, used in IPFS) is the go-to library for Go-based P2P:
    16→
    17→```go
    18→import (
    19→    libp2p "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p"
    20→    "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host"
    21→    "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/p2p/discovery/mdns"
    22→)
    23→```
    24→
    25→This gives you NAT traversal (hole punching), mDNS for local discovery, DHT for wide-area discovery, multiplexed streams over a single connection, and TLS/Noise encryption built in.
    26→
    27→### 2. Data Synchronization (CRDTs)
    28→
    29→Groove's killer feature was conflict-free offline editing. You'd use CRDTs (Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types) for this. Each peer maintains a local replica and merges changes automatically without conflicts:
    30→
    31→```go
    32→// Conceptual structure
    33→type SharedWorkspace struct {
    34→    ID        string
    35→    Documents map[string]*CRDTDocument
    36→    Members   []PeerID
    37→    Clock     VectorClock
    38→}
    39→```
    40→
    41→Look at libraries like **Automerge** (has Go bindings) or implement Lamport/vector clocks yourself for simpler cases.
    42→
    43→### 3. Core Components to Build
    44→
    45→Here's a roadmap of modules:
    46→
    47→**Peer Identity & Auth** — each peer generates a keypair (Ed25519). Identity = public key hash. Use libp2p's built-in identity system.
    48→
    49→**Workspace Manager** — create/join shared spaces. Each workspace has a membership list and a set of shared data objects. Invitations can be handled via signed tokens exchanged out-of-band.
    50→
    51→**Message Transport** — libp2p's pubsub (GossipSub) for group communication within a workspace:
    52→
    53→```go
    54→import pubsub "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub"
    55→
    56→// Each workspace becomes a pubsub topic
    57→topic, _ := ps.Join("workspace-" + workspaceID)
    58→sub, _ := topic.Subscribe()
    59→```
    60→
    61→**Persistence Layer** — local SQLite or Badger DB for offline storage. Sync state on reconnection using vector clocks or Merkle trees to efficiently detect differences.
    62→
    63→**Presence System** — periodic heartbeats over the workspace pubsub topic so peers know who's online.
    64→
    65→### 4. Suggested Project Structure
    66→
    67→```
    68→groove-go/
    69→├── cmd/groove/main.go        # Entry point
    70→├── internal/
    71→│   ├── node/                  # libp2p host setup, identity
    72→│   ├── workspace/             # Workspace CRUD, membership
    73→│   ├── sync/                  # CRDT engine, vector clocks
    74→│   ├── store/                 # Local persistence (SQLite/Badger)
    75→│   ├── transport/             # Pubsub, direct messaging
    76→│   └── presence/              # Online/offline tracking
    77→├── pkg/
    78→│   └── protocol/              # Protobuf message definitions
    79→└── go.mod
    80→```
    81→
    82→### 5. Key Libraries
    83→
    84→| Purpose | Library |
    85→|---|---|
    86→| P2P networking | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p` |
    87→| Pub/Sub | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub` |
    88→| DHT discovery | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht` |
    89→| Serialization | `google.golang.org/protobuf` |
    90→| Local DB | `github.com/dgraph-io/badger` or `modernc.org/sqlite` |
    91→| CRDTs | `github.com/automerge/automerge-go` |
    92→
    93→### 6. Where to Start (Incremental Build Order)
    94→
    95→1. **Boot a libp2p node** — connect two peers on a LAN via mDNS
    96→2. **Add pubsub** — send text messages between peers in a shared topic
    97→3. **Add persistence** — store messages locally, replay on reconnect
    98→4. **Add CRDT documents** — shared editable state that syncs without conflicts
    99→5. **Add workspace concept** — membership, invitations, multiple channels
   100→6. **Add presence** — heartbeats, online status
   101→7. **Add NAT traversal** — relay servers, hole punching for internet-wide connectivity
   102→
   103→### 7. Important Design Decisions
   104→
   105→**NAT Traversal** is the hardest real-world problem. libp2p supports AutoRelay and hole punching, but you'll likely want at least one public relay node as a fallback (could run on your `simplemap` VPS).
   106→
   107→**Security model**: Groove used a trust-on-first-use model with shared secrets per workspace. You could do similar — workspace creator generates a symmetric key, distributes it to invitees via a signed invitation.
   108→
   109→**Wire format**: Use Protobuf for all messages. It's compact, versioned, and Go has excellent support.
   110→
   111→## Interesting Angle for Your Context
   112→
   113→Given your Safecast work, a P2P architecture like this could be compelling for distributed sensor networks — imagine bGeigieZen devices forming mesh workspaces, syncing measurement data peer-to-peer without needing the central API, then eventually reconciling with the server when connectivity allows. The CRDT approach would handle intermittent connectivity gracefully.
   114→
   115→Want me to scaffold out any of these components in actual Go code, or dive deeper into a specific layer like the CRDT sync engine or the libp2p setup?

<system-reminder>
Whenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```

> AGENT

Here's a concrete plan to make GrooveGO a reality, following the incremental build order from the document:

---

## GrooveGO Implementation Plan

### Phase 1 — Foundation (Weeks 1–2)
**Goal: Two peers can find and talk to each other**

- [ ] Initialize Go module (`go mod init`)
- [ ] Set up project structure per the document (`cmd/`, `internal/`, `pkg/`)
- [ ] Implement `internal/node/` — boot a libp2p host with Ed25519 identity
- [ ] Add mDNS discovery so two peers on the same LAN auto-connect
- [ ] Verify with a simple ping/pong between two terminal instances

**Key library:** `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p`

---

### Phase 2 — Messaging (Week 3)
**Goal: Peers can send messages to a shared topic**

- [ ] Integrate GossipSub pubsub into `internal/transport/`
- [ ] Create a "workspace topic" abstraction (`workspace-<id>`)
- [ ] Simple CLI: join a workspace by ID, send/receive text messages

**Key library:** `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub`

---

### Phase 3 — Persistence (Week 4)
**Goal: Messages survive restarts and replay on reconnect**

- [ ] Implement `internal/store/` using Badger or SQLite
- [ ] Store all incoming/outgoing messages with timestamps
- [ ] On reconnect, request missed messages using a high-watermark from the peer

**Key library:** `github.com/dgraph-io/badger` or `modernc.org/sqlite`

---

### Phase 4 — Shared State / CRDTs (Weeks 5–6)
**Goal: Multiple peers can edit shared documents without conflicts**

- [ ] Implement `internal/sync/` with vector clocks
- [ ] Integrate Automerge-Go for CRDT document support
- [ ] Implement Merkle-diff sync: efficiently detect what changed since last sync
- [ ] Test: edit a shared JSON doc offline on two nodes, reconnect, verify merge

**Key library:** `github.com/automerge/automerge-go`

---

### Phase 5 — Workspace Concept (Week 7)
**Goal: Named workspaces with membership and invitations**

- [ ] Implement `internal/workspace/` — create, join, list workspaces
- [ ] Workspace creator generates a symmetric key, issues signed invitations
- [ ] Membership list stored as a CRDT (conflict-free additions)
- [ ] Define Protobuf message schemas in `pkg/protocol/`

**Key library:** `google.golang.org/protobuf`

---

### Phase 6 — Presence (Week 8)
**Goal: Know who is online**

- [ ] Implement `internal/presence/` — periodic heartbeat over workspace pubsub
- [ ] Track last-seen timestamps; expose online/offline status
- [ ] CLI shows member list with status indicators

---

### Phase 7 — Internet Connectivity (Weeks 9–10)
**Goal: Peers connect across NAT/internet, not just LAN**

- [ ] Add Kademlia DHT for wide-area peer discovery
- [ ] Configure AutoRelay — deploy a public relay on a VPS (e.g., your `simplemap` server)
- [ ] Enable hole punching (libp2p built-in)
- [ ] Test: two peers on different networks, different ISPs

**Key library:** `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht`

---

### Stretch / Safecast Integration
Once the core is solid, the document highlights a compelling angle: bGeigie devices forming **mesh workspaces** that sync radiation measurements P2P with CRDT conflict resolution, reconciling with the central Safecast API when connectivity returns. This would be a natural Phase 8.

---

### Suggested First Commit

```bash
mkdir -p groove-go/cmd/groove groove-go/internal/{node,workspace,sync,store,transport,presence} groove-go/pkg/protocol
cd groove-go
go mod init github.com/yourusername/groove-go
go get github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/node/node.go",
  "content": "package node\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\tlibp2p \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/crypto\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n)\n\n// Node wraps a libp2p host with GrooveGO-specific state.\ntype Node struct {\n\tHost host.Host\n\tID   peer.ID\n}\n\n// New creates a libp2p host with a fresh Ed25519 identity and listens on\n// a random TCP port. Call Close() when done.\nfunc New(ctx context.Context, listenPort int) (*Node, error) {\n\tpriv, _, err := crypto.GenerateEd25519Key(rand.Reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"generate identity: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlistenAddr := fmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/tcp/%d\", listenPort)\n\n\th, err := libp2p.New(\n\t\tlibp2p.Identity(priv),\n\t\tlibp2p.ListenAddrStrings(listenAddr),\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableNATService(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create host: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tn := &Node{\n\t\tHost: h,\n\t\tID:   h.ID(),\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[node] peer ID : %s\\n\", n.ID)\n\tfor _, addr := range h.Addrs() {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[node] listening: %s/p2p/%s\\n\", addr, n.ID)\n\t}\n\n\treturn n, nil\n}\n\n// Close shuts down the libp2p host.\nfunc (n *Node) Close() error {\n\treturn n.Host.Close()\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/node/mdns.go",
  "content": "package node\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/p2p/discovery/mdns\"\n)\n\nconst mdnsServiceTag = \"groove-go.local\"\n\n// discoveryNotifee receives mDNS peer-found events and connects to them.\ntype discoveryNotifee struct {\n\th host.Host\n}\n\nfunc (n *discoveryNotifee) HandlePeerFound(pi peer.AddrInfo) {\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] found peer: %s — connecting...\\n\", pi.ID)\n\tif err := n.h.Connect(n.h.Network().(*dummyNet).ctx, pi); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] connect error: %s\\n\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// StartMDNS starts a local mDNS discovery service on the given host.\n// Peers on the same LAN segment will be discovered and connected automatically.\nfunc StartMDNS(h host.Host) (mdns.Service, error) {\n\tsvc := mdns.NewMdnsService(h, mdnsServiceTag, &notifee{h: h})\n\tif err := svc.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"mdns start: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] service started (tag=%s)\\n\", mdnsServiceTag)\n\treturn svc, nil\n}\n\n// notifee satisfies mdns.Notifee and connects to discovered peers.\ntype notifee struct {\n\th host.Host\n}\n\nfunc (n *notifee) HandlePeerFound(pi peer.AddrInfo) {\n\tif pi.ID == n.h.ID() {\n\t\treturn // skip self\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] found peer: %s\\n\", pi.ID)\n\tif err := n.h.Connect(n.h.Network().Peers()[0:0:0], pi); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] connect failed: %s\\n\", err)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/node/mdns.go",

It connects to any peer discovered on the LAN.\ntype notifee struct {\n\tctx context.Context\n\th   host.Host\n}\n\nfunc (n *notifee) HandlePeerFound(pi peer.AddrInfo) {\n\tif pi.ID == n.h.ID() {\n\t\treturn // skip self\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] found peer: %s\\n\", pi.ID)\n\tif err := n.h.Connect(n.ctx, pi); err != nil {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] connect failed: %s\\n\", err)\n\t}\n}\n\n// StartMDNS starts a local mDNS discovery service on the given host.\n// Peers on the same LAN segment are discovered and connected automatically.\nfunc StartMDNS(ctx context.Context, h host.Host) (mdns.Service, error) {\n\tsvc := mdns.NewMdnsService(h, mdnsServiceTag, &notifee{ctx: ctx, h: h})\n\tif err := svc.Start(); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"mdns start: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[mdns] service started (tag=%s)\\n\", mdnsServiceTag)\n\treturn svc, nil\n}\n"

}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/.claude/projects/-home-rob-Documents-Safecast-GrooveGO/memory/project_groovego.md",
  "content": "---\nname: GrooveGO Project Plan\ndescription: Architecture and incremental build plan for GrooveGO — a P2P collaboration platform in Go inspired by Microsoft Groove\ntype: project\n---\n\nGrooveGO lives at `/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/` (module: `github.com/safecast/groove-go`).\n\n## Vision\nDecentralized P2P groupware (shared workspaces, real-time sync, offline capability, presence, E2E encryption) — no central server for core functionality. Inspired by Microsoft Groove. Built in Go using libp2p.\n\n## Incremental Build Phases\n\n| Phase | Status | What |\n|-------|--------|------|\n| 1 | ✅ Done | Boot libp2p node + mDNS LAN discovery (`internal/node`) |\n| 2 | pending | GossipSub pubsub — text messages between peers in a workspace topic |\n| 3 | pending | Persistence — Badger/SQLite local store, replay on reconnect |\n| 4 | pending | CRDT documents — shared editable state (Automerge-go) |\n| 5 | pending | Workspace concept — membership, invitations, multiple channels |\n| 6 | pending | Presence — heartbeats, online/offline status |\n| 7 | pending | NAT traversal — AutoRelay + hole punching for internet-wide peers |\n\n## Key Library Choices\n- P2P: `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p`\n- Pubsub: `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub` (GossipSub)\n- DHT: `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht`\n- Serialization: `google.golang.org/protobuf`\n- Local DB: `github.com/dgraph-io/badger` or `modernc.org/sqlite`\n- CRDTs: `github.com/automerge/automerge-go`\n\n## Project Structure\n```\ngroove-go/\n├── cmd/groove/main.go        # Entry point — flags, signal handling\n├── internal/\n│   ├── node/                 # libp2p host (Ed25519 identity), mDNS\n│   ├── workspace/            # Workspace CRUD, membership (Phase 5)\n│   ├── sync/                 # CRDT engine, vector clocks (Phase 4)\n│   ├── store/                # Local persistence (Phase 3)\n│   ├── transport/            # Pubsub, direct messaging (Phase 2)\n│   └── presence/             # Online/offline tracking (Phase 6)\n└── pkg/protocol/             # Protobuf message definitions\n```\n\n## Design Decisions\n- Identity: Ed25519 keypair per peer; peer ID = public key hash\n- Trust model: TOFU (trust on first use) + shared symmetric key per workspace\n- Wire format: Protobuf for all messages\n- NAT fallback: relay node can run on Safecast's `simplemap` VPS\n\n**Why:** Rob is from the open-source hardware / Safecast distributed sensor world; CRDT offline-sync and mesh networking map directly onto bGeigieZen use cases.\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/ARCHITECTURE.md",
  "content": "# GrooveGO — Architecture & Flow\n\n## Phase 1 — Node Boot & LAN Discovery\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant A as Peer A (groove node)\n    participant M as mDNS (LAN multicast)\n    participant B as Peer B (groove node)\n\n    A->>A: Generate Ed25519 keypair\n    A->>A: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)\n    A->>M: Advertise service tag \"groove-go.local\"\n\n    B->>B: Generate Ed25519 keypair\n    B->>B: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)\n    B->>M: Advertise service tag \"groove-go.local\"\n\n    M-->>A: Notify: Peer B found (AddrInfo)\n    M-->>B: Notify: Peer A found (AddrInfo)\n\n    A->>B: libp2p Connect (Noise/TLS handshake)\n    B-->>A: Connected ✓\n```\n\n## Full System — Component Interaction (All Phases)\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph Node[\"node (Phase 1)\"]\n        ID[\"Ed25519 Identity\\n(Peer ID = pubkey hash)\"]\n        HOST[\"libp2p Host\\n(TCP / QUIC / WebRTC)\"]\n        MDNS[\"mDNS Discovery\\n(LAN)\"]\n        DHT[\"Kademlia DHT\\n(WAN)\"]\n        RELAY[\"AutoRelay / Hole Punch\\n(NAT Traversal)\"]\n        ID --> HOST\n        HOST --> MDNS\n        HOST --> DHT\n        HOST --> RELAY\n    end\n\n    subgraph Transport[\"transport (Phase 2)\"]\n        PS[\"GossipSub PubSub\"]\n        DM[\"Direct Stream Messages\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Store[\"store (Phase 3)\"]\n        DB[\"Badger / SQLite\\n(local persistence)\"]\n        VCL[\"Vector Clocks\\n(sync state)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Sync[\"sync (Phase 4)\"]\n        CRDT[\"Automerge CRDT\\nDocuments\"]\n        MERGE[\"Conflict-Free Merge\\non Reconnect\"]\n        CRDT --> MERGE\n    end\n\n    subgraph Workspace[\"workspace (Phase 5)\"]\n        WS[\"Workspace Manager\\n(create / join)\"]\n        MEM[\"Membership List\\n(signed invitations)\"]\n        SYMKEY[\"Symmetric Key\\n(per workspace)\"]\n        WS --> MEM\n        WS --> SYMKEY\n    end\n\n    subgraph Presence[\"presence (Phase 6)\"]\n        HB[\"Heartbeat\\n(periodic pubsub ping)\"]\n        STATUS[\"Online / Offline\\nStatus Map\"]\n        HB --> STATUS\n    end\n\n    HOST --> PS\n    HOST --> DM\n    PS --> DB\n    DM --> DB\n    DB --> VCL\n    VCL --> MERGE\n    PS --> WS\n    PS --> HB\n    WS --> CRDT\n```\n\n## Data Sync — Offline & Reconnect Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant A as Peer A (online)\n    participant DB_A as Store A\n    participant DB_B as Store B\n    participant B as Peer B (was offline)\n\n    Note over B: Goes offline\n    A->>DB_A: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)\n    B->>DB_B: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)\n\n    Note over B: Comes back online\n    B->>A: Connect (mDNS / DHT)\n    A->>B: Exchange vector clocks\n    B->>A: Send missing ops (delta sync)\n    A->>B: Send missing ops (delta sync)\n    A->>A: Merge (conflict-free)\n    B->>B: Merge (conflict-free)\n    Note over A,B: Consistent state ✓\n```\n"
}
```

1	# GrooveGO — Architecture & Flow
2	
3	## Phase 1 — Node Boot & LAN Discovery
4	
5	```mermaid
6	sequenceDiagram
7	    participant A as Peer A (groove node)
8	    participant M as mDNS (LAN multicast)
9	    participant B as Peer B (groove node)
10	
11	    A->>A: Generate Ed25519 keypair
12	    A->>A: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)
13	    A->>M: Advertise service tag "groove-go.local"
14	
15	    B->>B: Generate Ed25519 keypair
16	    B->>B: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)
17	    B->>M: Advertise service tag "groove-go.local"
18	
19	    M-->>A: Notify: Peer B found (AddrInfo)
20	    M-->>B: Notify: Peer A found (AddrInfo)
21	
22	    A->>B: libp2p Connect (Noise/TLS handshake)
23	    B-->>A: Connected ✓
24	```
25	
26	## Full System — Component Interaction (All Phases)
27	
28	```mermaid
29	flowchart TD
30	    subgraph Node["node (Phase 1)"]
31	        ID["Ed25519 Identity\n(Peer ID = pubkey hash)"]
32	        HOST["libp2p Host\n(TCP / QUIC / WebRTC)"]
33	        MDNS["mDNS Discovery\n(LAN)"]
34	        DHT["Kademlia DHT\n(WAN)"]
35	        RELAY["AutoRelay / Hole Punch\n(NAT Traversal)"]
36	        ID --> HOST
37	        HOST --> MDNS
38	        HOST --> DHT
39	        HOST --> RELAY
40	    end
41	
42	    subgraph Transport["transport (Phase 2)"]
43	        PS["GossipSub PubSub"]
44	        DM["Direct Stream Messages"]
45	    end
46	
47	    subgraph Store["store (Phase 3)"]
48	        DB["Badger / SQLite\n(local persistence)"]
49	        VCL["Vector Clocks\n(sync state)"]
50	    end
51	
52	    subgraph Sync["sync (Phase 4)"]
53	        CRDT["Automerge CRDT\nDocuments"]
54	        MERGE["Conflict-Free Merge\non Reconnect"]
55	        CRDT --> MERGE
56	    end
57	
58	    subgraph Workspace["workspace (Phase 5)"]
59	        WS["Workspace Manager\n(create / join)"]
60	        MEM["Membership List\n(signed invitations)"]
61	        SYMKEY["Symmetric Key\n(per workspace)"]
62	        WS --> MEM
63	        WS --> SYMKEY
64	    end
65	
66	    subgraph Presence["presence (Phase 6)"]
67	        HB["Heartbeat\n(periodic pubsub ping)"]
68	        STATUS["Online / Offline\nStatus Map"]
69	        HB --> STATUS
70	    end
71	
72	    HOST --> PS
73	    HOST --> DM
74	    PS --> DB
75	    DM --> DB
76	    DB --> VCL
77	    VCL --> MERGE
78	    PS --> WS
79	    PS --> HB
80	    WS --> CRDT
81	```
82	
83	## Data Sync — Offline & Reconnect Flow
84	
85	```mermaid
86	sequenceDiagram
87	    participant A as Peer A (online)
88	    participant DB_A as Store A
89	    participant DB_B as Store B
90	    participant B as Peer B (was offline)
91	
92	    Note over B: Goes offline
93	    A->>DB_A: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)
94	    B->>DB_B: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)
95	
96	    Note over B: Comes back online
97	    B->>A: Connect (mDNS / DHT)
98	    A->>B: Exchange vector clocks
99	    B->>A: Send missing ops (delta sync)
100	    A->>B: Send missing ops (delta sync)
101	    A->>A: Merge (conflict-free)
102	    B->>B: Merge (conflict-free)
103	    Note over A,B: Consistent state ✓
104	```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/README.md",
  "content": "# GrooveGO\n\nA peer-to-peer collaboration platform in Go, inspired by Microsoft Groove. Decentralized shared workspaces with real-time sync, offline capability, presence awareness, and end-to-end encryption — no central server required.\n\n## Vision\n\nGrooveGO brings back the best of Groove's architecture on modern P2P primitives:\n\n- **Decentralized** — peers connect directly, no central broker\n- **Offline-first** — CRDT-based sync means you keep working when disconnected\n- **Encrypted** — Ed25519 identity + per-workspace symmetric keys\n- **LAN & WAN** — mDNS for local discovery, Kademlia DHT for internet-wide peering\n\n## Build Phases\n\n| Phase | Status | Feature |\n|-------|--------|---------|\n| 1 | ✅ Done | libp2p node bootstrap + mDNS LAN discovery |\n| 2 | 🔜 Next | GossipSub pubsub — text messaging within workspace topics |\n| 3 | ⬜ | Persistence — Badger/SQLite local store, replay on reconnect |\n| 4 | ⬜ | CRDT documents — conflict-free shared state (Automerge) |\n| 5 | ⬜ | Workspace manager — membership, signed invitations |\n| 6 | ⬜ | Presence — heartbeats, online/offline status |\n| 7 | ⬜ | NAT traversal — AutoRelay + hole punching for WAN peers |\n\n## Project Structure\n\n```\ngroove-go/\n├── cmd/groove/main.go        # Entry point (--port flag, graceful shutdown)\n├── internal/\n│   ├── node/                 # libp2p host, Ed25519 identity, mDNS\n│   ├── workspace/            # Workspace CRUD, membership (Phase 5)\n│   ├── sync/                 # CRDT engine, vector clocks (Phase 4)\n│   ├── store/                # Local persistence (Phase 3)\n│   ├── transport/            # GossipSub, direct messaging (Phase 2)\n│   └── presence/             # Online/offline tracking (Phase 6)\n└── pkg/protocol/             # Protobuf message definitions\n```\n\n## Getting Started\n\n**Requirements:** Go 1.22+\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/robouden/GrooveGO.git\ncd GrooveGO/groove-go\ngo mod download\n```\n\n**Run two nodes on the same LAN** — they will discover each other automatically via mDNS:\n\n```bash\n# Terminal 1\ngo run ./cmd/groove --port 9000\n\n# Terminal 2\ngo run ./cmd/groove --port 9001\n```\n\nEach node prints its peer ID and listen address on startup. Once discovered, peers connect automatically.\n\n## Architecture\n\nSee [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) for full system diagrams, or open the standalone Mermaid files in [diagrams/](diagrams/):\n\n- [01-node-boot-lan-discovery.mmd](diagrams/01-node-boot-lan-discovery.mmd) — Phase 1 boot & mDNS sequence\n- [02-full-system-components.mmd](diagrams/02-full-system-components.mmd) — Full component flowchart (all phases)\n- [03-offline-sync-reconnect.mmd](diagrams/03-offline-sync-reconnect.mmd) — Offline CRDT sync & reconnect flow\n\n## Key Libraries\n\n| Purpose | Library |\n|---------|---------|\n| P2P networking | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p` |\n| Pub/Sub | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub` |\n| DHT discovery | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht` |\n| Serialization | `google.golang.org/protobuf` |\n| Local DB | `github.com/dgraph-io/badger` |\n| CRDTs | `github.com/automerge/automerge-go` |\n\n## Connection to Safecast\n\nThe CRDT + offline-first design maps naturally onto distributed sensor networks. bGeigieZen devices could form mesh workspaces, syncing measurement data peer-to-peer without the central API, then reconciling with the server when connectivity allows.\n\n## License\n\nMIT\n"
}
```

  "old_string": "## Architecture\n\nSee [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md) for full system diagrams, or open the standalone Mermaid files in [diagrams/](diagrams/):\n\n- [01-node-boot-lan-discovery.mmd](diagrams/01-node-boot-lan-discovery.mmd) — Phase 1 boot & mDNS sequence\n- [02-full-system-components.mmd](diagrams/02-full-system-components.mmd) — Full component flowchart (all phases)\n- [03-offline-sync-reconnect.mmd](diagrams/03-offline-sync-reconnect.mmd) — Offline CRDT sync & reconnect flow",
  "new_string": "## Architecture\n\n### Phase 1 — Node Boot & LAN Discovery\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant A as Peer A (groove node)\n    participant M as mDNS (LAN multicast)\n    participant B as Peer B (groove node)\n\n    A->>A: Generate Ed25519 keypair\n    A->>A: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)\n    A->>M: Advertise service tag \"groove-go.local\"\n\n    B->>B: Generate Ed25519 keypair\n    B->>B: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)\n    B->>M: Advertise service tag \"groove-go.local\"\n\n    M-->>A: Notify: Peer B found (AddrInfo)\n    M-->>B: Notify: Peer A found (AddrInfo)\n\n    A->>B: libp2p Connect (Noise/TLS handshake)\n    B-->>A: Connected ✓\n```\n\n### Full System — Component Interaction (All Phases)\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart TD\n    subgraph Node[\"node (Phase 1)\"]\n        ID[\"Ed25519 Identity\\n(Peer ID = pubkey hash)\"]\n        HOST[\"libp2p Host\\n(TCP / QUIC / WebRTC)\"]\n        MDNS[\"mDNS Discovery\\n(LAN)\"]\n        DHT[\"Kademlia DHT\\n(WAN)\"]\n        RELAY[\"AutoRelay / Hole Punch\\n(NAT Traversal)\"]\n        ID --> HOST\n        HOST --> MDNS\n        HOST --> DHT\n        HOST --> RELAY\n    end\n\n    subgraph Transport[\"transport (Phase 2)\"]\n        PS[\"GossipSub PubSub\"]\n        DM[\"Direct Stream Messages\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Store[\"store (Phase 3)\"]\n        DB[\"Badger / SQLite\\n(local persistence)\"]\n        VCL[\"Vector Clocks\\n(sync state)\"]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Sync[\"sync (Phase 4)\"]\n        CRDT[\"Automerge CRDT\\nDocuments\"]\n        MERGE[\"Conflict-Free Merge\\non Reconnect\"]\n        CRDT --> MERGE\n    end\n\n    subgraph Workspace[\"workspace (Phase 5)\"]\n        WS[\"Workspace Manager\\n(create / join)\"]\n        MEM[\"Membership List\\n(signed invitations)\"]\n        SYMKEY[\"Symmetric Key\\n(per workspace)\"]\n        WS --> MEM\n        WS --> SYMKEY\n    end\n\n    subgraph Presence[\"presence (Phase 6)\"]\n        HB[\"Heartbeat\\n(periodic pubsub ping)\"]\n        STATUS[\"Online / Offline\\nStatus Map\"]\n        HB --> STATUS\n    end\n\n    HOST --> PS\n    HOST --> DM\n    PS --> DB\n    DM --> DB\n    DB --> VCL\n    VCL --> MERGE\n    PS --> WS\n    PS --> HB\n    WS --> CRDT\n```\n\n### Data Sync — Offline & Reconnect Flow\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    participant A as Peer A (online)\n    participant DB_A as Store A\n    participant DB_B as Store B\n    participant B as Peer B (was offline)\n\n    Note over B: Goes offline\n    A->>DB_A: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)\n    B->>DB_B: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)\n\n    Note over B: Comes back online\n    B->>A: Connect (mDNS / DHT)\n    A->>B: Exchange vector clocks\n    B->>A: Send missing ops (delta sync)\n    A->>B: Send missing ops (delta sync)\n    A->>A: Merge (conflict-free)\n    B->>B: Merge (conflict-free)\n    Note over A,B: Consistent state ✓\n```\n\nFull standalone diagram files are also in [diagrams/](diagrams/)."
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/transport/pubsub.go",
  "content": "package transport\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\tpubsub \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub\"\n)\n\n// Message is the wire format for all workspace messages.\ntype Message struct {\n\tFrom      string    `json:\"from\"`\n\tWorkspace string    `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tTimestamp time.Time `json:\"ts\"`\n}\n\n// Workspace represents a joined pubsub topic (a shared channel).\ntype Workspace struct {\n\tname  string\n\tself  peer.ID\n\ttopic *pubsub.Topic\n\tsub   *pubsub.Subscription\n\tps    *pubsub.PubSub\n}\n\n// NewGossipSub creates a GossipSub router attached to the given host.\nfunc NewGossipSub(ctx context.Context, h host.Host) (*pubsub.PubSub, error) {\n\tps, err := pubsub.NewGossipSub(ctx, h)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"gossipsub: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Println(\"[transport] GossipSub router started\")\n\treturn ps, nil\n}\n\n// JoinWorkspace joins (or creates) a pubsub topic for the named workspace.\nfunc JoinWorkspace(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, name string) (*Workspace, error) {\n\ttopic, err := ps.Join(\"workspace-\" + name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"join topic: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tsub, err := topic.Subscribe()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"subscribe: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[transport] joined workspace: %s\\n\", name)\n\treturn &Workspace{\n\t\tname:  name,\n\t\tself:  self,\n\t\ttopic: topic,\n\t\tsub:   sub,\n\t\tps:    ps,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// Publish sends a message to the workspace topic.\nfunc (w *Workspace) Publish(ctx context.Context, body string) error {\n\tmsg := Message{\n\t\tFrom:      w.self.String(),\n\t\tWorkspace: w.name,\n\t\tBody:      body,\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t}\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(msg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn w.topic.Publish(ctx, data)\n}\n\n// ReadLoop blocks and prints every message received on the workspace topic.\n// It returns when ctx is cancelled or the subscription is closed.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip our own messages — we already echo them on send.\n\t\tif m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg Message\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] %s: %s\\n\", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)\n\t}\n}\n\n// ListPeers returns the peer IDs currently subscribed to this workspace topic.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ListPeers() []peer.ID {\n\treturn w.ps.ListPeers(\"workspace-\" + w.name)\n}\n\nfunc shortID(id string) string {\n\tif len(id) > 12 {\n\t\treturn id[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/cmd/groove/main.go",
  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"bufio\"\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"strings\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\tworkspace := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Workspace name to join\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// GossipSub router\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Join workspace topic\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Receive messages in background\n\tgo ws.ReadLoop(ctx)\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[groove] type a message and press Enter to send (workspace: %s)\\n\", *workspace)\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove] Ctrl+C to quit\")\n\n\t// Read stdin and publish\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\n\tscanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)\n\tinput   := make(chan string)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfor scanner.Scan() {\n\t\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(scanner.Text())\n\t\t\tif line != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tinput <- line\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tclose(input)\n\t}()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase line, ok := <-input:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := ws.Publish(ctx, line); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[error] publish: %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] me: %s\\n\", *workspace, line)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase <-quit:\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"[groove] shutting down\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/Makefile",
  "content": "BINARY  := groove\nMODULE  := github.com/safecast/groove-go\nCMD     := ./cmd/groove\nOUTDIR  := dist\n\n.PHONY: all build linux mac windows clean\n\nall: linux mac windows\n\nbuild:\n\tgo build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY) $(CMD)\n\nlinux:\n\tGOOS=linux   GOARCH=amd64 go build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY)-linux-amd64   $(CMD)\n\tGOOS=linux   GOARCH=arm64 go build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY)-linux-arm64   $(CMD)\n\nmac:\n\tGOOS=darwin  GOARCH=amd64 go build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY)-mac-amd64     $(CMD)\n\tGOOS=darwin  GOARCH=arm64 go build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY)-mac-arm64     $(CMD)\n\nwindows:\n\tGOOS=windows GOARCH=amd64 go build -o $(OUTDIR)/$(BINARY)-windows-amd64.exe $(CMD)\n\nclean:\n\trm -rf $(OUTDIR)\n"
}

> AGENT

Phase 2 done — `f38b156` pushed. Here's what's new:

**What was built:**
- [groove-go/internal/transport/pubsub.go](groove-go/internal/transport/pubsub.go) — GossipSub router, workspace topic, publish/subscribe with JSON messages
- [groove-go/cmd/groove/main.go](groove-go/cmd/groove/main.go) — type a message, hit Enter, it broadcasts to all peers in the workspace
- [groove-go/Makefile](groove-go/Makefile) — cross-compile for all platforms

**To try it:**
```bash
cd groove-go

# Terminal 1
go run ./cmd/groove --port 9000 --workspace general

# Terminal 2
go run ./cmd/groove --port 9001 --workspace general

# Type a message in either terminal — it appears in the other
```

**To build release binaries for all platforms:**
```bash
make all   # produces dist/groove-linux-amd64, groove-mac-arm64, groove-windows-amd64.exe, etc.
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/store/store.go",
  "content": "package store\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\tbadger \"github.com/dgraph-io/badger/v4\"\n)\n\n// Store wraps a Badger key-value database for local message persistence.\ntype Store struct {\n\tdb *badger.DB\n}\n\n// Message mirrors transport.Message — duplicated here to avoid a circular dep.\ntype Message struct {\n\tFrom      string    `json:\"from\"`\n\tWorkspace string    `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tTimestamp time.Time `json:\"ts\"`\n}\n\n// Open opens (or creates) a Badger database at the given directory path.\nfunc Open(dir string) (*Store, error) {\n\topts := badger.DefaultOptions(dir).WithLogger(nil)\n\tdb, err := badger.Open(opts)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"store open: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[store] opened at %s\\n\", dir)\n\treturn &Store{db: db}, nil\n}\n\n// Close flushes and closes the database.\nfunc (s *Store) Close() error {\n\treturn s.db.Close()\n}\n\n// Save persists a message. Key format: msg/<workspace>/<unix-nano>/<from>\nfunc (s *Store) Save(msg Message) error {\n\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"msg/%s/%d/%s\", msg.Workspace, msg.Timestamp.UnixNano(), msg.From)\n\tval, err := json.Marshal(msg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn s.db.Update(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {\n\t\treturn txn.Set([]byte(key), val)\n\t})\n}\n\n// History returns all stored messages for a workspace, oldest first.\nfunc (s *Store) History(workspace string) ([]Message, error) {\n\tprefix := []byte(\"msg/\" + workspace + \"/\")\n\tvar msgs []Message\n\n\terr := s.db.View(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {\n\t\topts := badger.DefaultIteratorOptions\n\t\topts.Prefix = prefix\n\t\tit := txn.NewIterator(opts)\n\t\tdefer it.Close()\n\n\t\tfor it.Rewind(); it.Valid(); it.Next() {\n\t\t\titem := it.Item()\n\t\t\tif err := item.Value(func(v []byte) error {\n\t\t\t\tvar m Message\n\t\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(v, &m); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tmsgs = append(msgs, m)\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\treturn msgs, err\n}\n"
}

```
1	package transport
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"encoding/json"
6		"fmt"
7		"time"
8	
9		"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host"
10		"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer"
11		pubsub "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub"
12	)
13	
14	// Message is the wire format for all workspace messages.
15	type Message struct {
16		From      string    `json:"from"`
17		Workspace string    `json:"workspace"`
18		Body      string    `json:"body"`
19		Timestamp time.Time `json:"ts"`
20	}
21	
22	// Workspace represents a joined pubsub topic (a shared channel).
23	type Workspace struct {
24		name  string
25		self  peer.ID
26		topic *pubsub.Topic
27		sub   *pubsub.Subscription
28		ps    *pubsub.PubSub
29	}
30	
31	// NewGossipSub creates a GossipSub router attached to the given host.
32	func NewGossipSub(ctx context.Context, h host.Host) (*pubsub.PubSub, error) {
33		ps, err := pubsub.NewGossipSub(ctx, h)
34		if err != nil {
35			return nil, fmt.Errorf("gossipsub: %w", err)
36		}
37		fmt.Println("[transport] GossipSub router started")
38		return ps, nil
39	}
40	
41	// JoinWorkspace joins (or creates) a pubsub topic for the named workspace.
42	func JoinWorkspace(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, name string) (*Workspace, error) {
43		topic, err := ps.Join("workspace-" + name)
44		if err != nil {
45			return nil, fmt.Errorf("join topic: %w", err)
46		}
47	
48		sub, err := topic.Subscribe()
49		if err != nil {
50			return nil, fmt.Errorf("subscribe: %w", err)
51		}
52	
53		fmt.Printf("[transport] joined workspace: %s\n", name)
54		return &Workspace{
55			name:  name,
56			self:  self,
57			topic: topic,
58			sub:   sub,
59			ps:    ps,
60		}, nil
61	}
62	
63	// Publish sends a message to the workspace topic.
64	func (w *Workspace) Publish(ctx context.Context, body string) error {
65		msg := Message{
66			From:      w.self.String(),
67			Workspace: w.name,
68			Body:      body,
69			Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
70		}
71		data, err := json.Marshal(msg)
72		if err != nil {
73			return err
74		}
75		return w.topic.Publish(ctx, data)
76	}
77	
78	// ReadLoop blocks and prints every message received on the workspace topic.
79	// It returns when ctx is cancelled or the subscription is closed.
80	func (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {
81		for {
82			m, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)
83			if err != nil {
84				return
85			}
86			// Skip our own messages — we already echo them on send.
87			if m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {
88				continue
89			}
90			var msg Message
91			if err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {
92				continue
93			}
94			fmt.Printf("[%s] %s: %s\n", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)
95		}
96	}
97	
98	// ListPeers returns the peer IDs currently subscribed to this workspace topic.
99	func (w *Workspace) ListPeers() []peer.ID {
100		return w.ps.ListPeers("workspace-" + w.name)
101	}
102	
103	func shortID(id string) string {
104		if len(id) > 12 {
105			return id[:12]
106		}
107		return id
108	}
109

context.Context, h host.Host) (*pubsub.PubSub, error) {\n\tps, err := pubsub.NewGossipSub(ctx, h)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"gossipsub: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Println(\"[transport] GossipSub router started\")\n\treturn ps, nil\n}\n\n// JoinWorkspace joins (or creates) a pubsub topic for the named workspace.\n// Pass a non-nil store to enable persistence.\nfunc JoinWorkspace(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, name string, s *store.Store) (*Workspace, error) {\n\ttopic, err := ps.Join(\"workspace-\" + name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"join topic: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tsub, err := topic.Subscribe()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"subscribe: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[transport] joined workspace: %s\\n\", name)\n\treturn &Workspace{\n\t\tname:  name,\n\t\tself:  self,\n\t\ttopic: topic,\n\t\tsub:   sub,\n\t\tps:    ps,\n\t\tstore: s,\n\t}, nil\n}\n\n// Publish sends a message to the workspace topic and persists it locally.\nfunc (w *Workspace) Publish(ctx context.Context, body string) error {\n\tmsg := Message{\n\t\tFrom:      w.self.String(),\n\t\tWorkspace: w.name,\n\t\tBody:      body,\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t}\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(msg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif err := w.topic.Publish(ctx, data); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ReadLoop blocks and prints every incoming message, persisting each one.\n// Returns when ctx is cancelled.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip our own messages — already echoed and saved on Publish.\n\t\tif m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg Message\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] %s: %s\\n\", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)\n\t\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// ListPeers returns the peer IDs currently subscribed to this workspace topic.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ListPeers() []peer.ID {\n\treturn w.ps.ListPeers(\"workspace-\" + w.name)\n}\n\nfunc shortID(id string) string {\n\tif len(id) > 12 {\n\t\treturn id[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n"
}

main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\tworkspace := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Workspace name to join\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Directory for local message store\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Local persistence\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(*dataDir, *workspace))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer s.Close()\n\n\t// Replay history from previous sessions\n\thistory, err := s.History(*workspace)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif len(history) > 0 {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[store] replaying %d message(s) from previous session:\\n\", len(history))\n\t\tfor _, m := range history {\n\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"  [%s] %s: %s\\n\", m.Workspace, shortID(m.From), m.Body)\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Println(\"[store] --- end of history ---\")\n\t}\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// GossipSub router\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Join workspace topic (with store for persistence)\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace, s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Receive messages in background\n\tgo ws.ReadLoop(ctx)\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[groove] workspace: %s — type a message and press Enter\\n\", *workspace)\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove] Ctrl+C to quit\")\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\n\tscanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)\n\tinput   := make(chan string)\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tfor scanner.Scan() {\n\t\t\tline := strings.TrimSpace(scanner.Text())\n\t\t\tif line != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tinput <- line\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\tclose(input)\n\t}()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase line, ok := <-input:\n\t\t\tif !ok {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := ws.Publish(ctx, line); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[error] publish: %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t} else {\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] me: %s\\n\", *workspace, line)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\tcase <-quit:\n\t\t\tfmt.Println(\"[groove] shutting down\")\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc shortID(id string) string {\n\tif len(id) > 12 {\n\t\treturn id[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"

> AGENT

Phase 3 pushed (`3e66e63`). Messages now survive restarts — on launch you'll see your full chat history before reconnecting.

---

**On the UI request** — great idea. The cleanest approach for a cross-platform terminal UI is **[Bubble Tea](https://github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea)** (Go, works on Linux/macOS/Windows). It gives you a proper chat window: a scrollable message list at the top, an input box at the bottom, like this:

```
┌─────────────────────────────┐
│ workspace: general          │
│                             │
│  12:03 me: hello            │
│  12:04 QmXf3r...: hi there  │
│                             │
│> type here and press Enter_ │
└─────────────────────────────┘
```

Want me to build that now as a `cmd/groove-ui` alongside the existing CLI?

> DEVELOPER

Give it a try..

------------------------------------------\n\n// incomingMsg carries a chat line received from the pubsub layer.\ntype incomingMsg struct {\n\tfrom      string\n\tbody      string\n\tworkspace string\n\tts        time.Time\n}\n\n// peerCountMsg updates the connected peer count in the status bar.\ntype peerCountMsg int\n\n// waitForMessage is a tea.Cmd that blocks on the incoming channel.\nfunc waitForMessage(ch <-chan incomingMsg) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn func() tea.Msg {\n\t\treturn <-ch\n\t}\n}\n\n// pollPeers periodically refreshes the peer count.\nfunc pollPeers(getPeers func() int) tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Tick(2*time.Second, func(_ time.Time) tea.Msg {\n\t\treturn peerCountMsg(getPeers())\n\t})\n}\n\n// ---- Model -----------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntype model struct {\n\tworkspace string\n\tselfID    string\n\tviewport  viewport.Model\n\ttextarea  textarea.Model\n\tmessages  []string\n\tincoming  <-chan incomingMsg\n\tgetPeers  func() int\n\tpeers     int\n\tpublish   func(context.Context, string) error\n\tctx       context.Context\n\twidth     int\n\theight    int\n\tready     bool\n}\n\nfunc newModel(\n\tctx context.Context,\n\tworkspace, selfID string,\n\tincoming <-chan incomingMsg,\n\tgetPeers func() int,\n\tpublish func(context.Context, string) error,\n\thistory []incomingMsg,\n) model {\n\tta := textarea.New()\n\tta.Placeholder = \"Type a message…\"\n\tta.Focus()\n\tta.CharLimit = 500\n\tta.SetWidth(80)\n\tta.SetHeight(3)\n\tta.ShowLineNumbers = false\n\tta.KeyMap.InsertNewline.SetEnabled(false) // Enter sends, not newlines\n\n\tm := model{\n\t\tworkspace: workspace,\n\t\tselfID:    selfID,\n\t\ttextarea:  ta,\n\t\tincoming:  incoming,\n\t\tgetPeers:  getPeers,\n\t\tpublish:   publish,\n\t\tctx:       ctx,\n\t}\n\n\t// Pre-load history\n\tfor _, h := range history {\n\t\tm.messages = append(m.messages, m.formatLine(h.from, h.body, h.ts, false))\n\t}\n\treturn m\n}\n\nfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n\treturn tea.Batch(\n\t\ttextarea.Blink,\n\t\twaitForMessage(m.incoming),\n\t\tpollPeers(m.getPeers),\n\t)\n}\n\nfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n\tvar (\n\t\ttaCmd tea.Cmd\n\t\tvpCmd tea.Cmd\n\t)\n\n\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n\n\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n\t\tinputHeight := 5 // textarea + border\n\t\theaderHeight := 2\n\t\tvpHeight := m.height - inputHeight - headerHeight\n\t\tif vpHeight < 1 {\n\t\t\tvpHeight = 1\n\t\t}\n\t\tif !m.ready {\n\t\t\tm.viewport = viewport.New(m.width, vpHeight)\n\t\t\tm.viewport.SetContent(m.renderMessages())\n\t\t\tm.ready = true\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tm.viewport.Width = m.width\n\t\t\tm.viewport.Height = vpHeight\n\t\t}\n\t\tm.textarea.SetWidth(m.width - 4)\n\n\tcase incomingMsg:\n\t\tline := m.formatLine(msg.from, msg.body, msg.ts, false)\n\t\tm.messages = append(m.messages, line)\n\t\tm.viewport.SetContent(m.renderMessages())\n\t\tm.viewport.GotoBottom()\n\t\treturn m, waitForMessage(m.incoming) // wait for next message\n\n\tcase peerCountMsg:\n\t\tm.peers = int(msg)\n\t\treturn m, pollPeers(m.getPeers)\n\n\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n\t\tswitch msg.Type {\n\t\tcase tea.KeyCtrlC, tea.KeyEsc:\n\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n\n\t\tcase tea.KeyEnter:\n\t\t\ttext := strings.TrimSpace(m.textarea.Value())\n\t\t\tif text != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t_ = m.publish(m.ctx, text)\n\t\t\t\tline := m.formatLine(m.selfID, text, time.Now(), true)\n\t\t\t\tm.messages = append(m.messages, line)\n\t\t\t\tm.viewport.SetContent(m.renderMessages())\n\t\t\t\tm.viewport.GotoBottom()\n\t\t\t\tm.textarea.Reset()\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tm.textarea, taCmd = m.textarea.Update(msg)\n\tm.viewport, vpCmd = m.viewport.Update(msg)\n\treturn m, tea.Batch(taCmd, vpCmd)\n}\n\nfunc (m model) View() string {\n\tif !m.ready {\n\t\treturn \"\\n  Initialising…\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Header\n\tpeerInfo := peerStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\"  %d peer(s) connected\", m.peers))\n\theader := titleStyle.Render(\"GrooveGO  #\"+m.workspace) + peerInfo\n\tdivider := dividerStyle.Render(strings.Repeat(\"─\", m.width))\n\n\t// Input box\n\tinput := inputBorderStyle.Render(m.textarea.View())\n\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s\\n%s\\n%s\\n%s\",\n\t\theader,\n\t\tdivider,\n\t\tm.viewport.View(),\n\t\tinput,\n\t)\n}\n\n// formatLine renders a single chat line.\nfunc (m model) formatLine(from, body string, ts time.Time, isSelf bool) string {\n\tt := timestampStyle.Render(ts.Format(\"15:04\"))\n\tvar name string\n\tif isSelf {\n\t\tname = senderSelfStyle.Render(\"me\")\n\t} else {\n\t\tname = senderPeerStyle.Render(shortID(from))\n\t}\n\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%s  %s: %s\", t, name, body)\n}\n\nfunc (m model) renderMessages() string {\n\tif len(m.messages) == 0 {\n\t\treturn peerStyle.Render(\"  No messages yet — say hello!\")\n\t}\n\treturn strings.Join(m.messages, \"\\n\")\n}\n\nfunc shortID(id string) string {\n\tif len(id) > 12 {\n\t\treturn id[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n"
}

main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\tworkspace := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Workspace name to join\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Directory for local message store\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Local persistence\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(*dataDir, *workspace))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer s.Close()\n\n\t// Load history for the TUI\n\thistory, err := s.History(*workspace)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\thistMsgs := make([]incomingMsg, len(history))\n\tfor i, h := range history {\n\t\thistMsgs[i] = incomingMsg{\n\t\t\tfrom:      h.From,\n\t\t\tbody:      h.Body,\n\t\t\tworkspace: h.Workspace,\n\t\t\tts:        h.Timestamp,\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// GossipSub router\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Join workspace topic\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace, s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Bridge pubsub → TUI via buffered channel\n\tincoming := make(chan incomingMsg, 64)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tws.ReadLoopChan(ctx, func(from, body, wsName string, ts interface{ String() string }) {\n\t\t\t// ts is time.Time — cast via transport.Message helpers\n\t\t})\n\t}()\n\t// Use the channel-based reader\n\tgo ws.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(m transport.Message) {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase incoming <- incomingMsg{from: m.From, body: m.Body, workspace: m.Workspace, ts: m.Timestamp}:\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tgetPeers := func() int { return len(ws.ListPeers()) }\n\n\tm := newModel(ctx, *workspace, n.ID.String(), incoming, getPeers, ws.Publish, histMsgs)\n\n\tp := tea.NewProgram(m, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion())\n\tif _, err := p.Run(); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}

  "old_string": "// ReadLoop blocks and prints every incoming message, persisting each one.\n// Returns when ctx is cancelled.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip our own messages — already echoed and saved on Publish.\n\t\tif m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg Message\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] %s: %s\\n\", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)\n\t\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}",
  "new_string": "// ReadLoop blocks and prints every incoming message, persisting each one.\n// Returns when ctx is cancelled.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tw.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(msg Message) {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] %s: %s\\n\", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)\n\t})\n}\n\n// ReadLoopInto blocks and calls fn for every incoming message from other peers.\n// Messages are also persisted to the store if one is set.\n// Returns when ctx is cancelled.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoopInto(ctx context.Context, fn func(Message)) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Skip our own messages.\n\t\tif m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg Message\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))\n\t\t}\n\t\tfn(msg)\n\t}\n}"
}

> AGENT

Pushed `fbf8e08`. Here's what you've got:

**To run the TUI:**
```bash
cd groove-go
go run ./cmd/groove-ui --workspace general
```

**What it looks like:**
```
 GrooveGO  #general   2 peer(s) connected
────────────────────────────────────────────
 12:03  me: hello everyone
 12:04  QmXf3rAb12cd: hi there!
 12:05  me: anyone from Safecast?

╭──────────────────────────────────────────╮
│ Type a message…                          │
╰──────────────────────────────────────────╯
```

**Controls:**
- `Enter` — send message
- `↑/↓` or scroll — navigate message history
- `Esc` / `Ctrl+C` — quit

The plain CLI (`cmd/groove`) is still there for headless/server use. Ready for Phase 4 (CRDT documents) or want to try running it first?

> DEVELOPER

can we make someting like that in a browser?

> AGENT

Yes — cleanest approach: a small HTTP server embedded in the Go node that serves a single HTML page and uses **WebSockets** to bridge the browser ↔ pubsub. No npm, no build step, the HTML is baked into the binary.

```
Browser (HTML + JS)
      ↕  WebSocket
  Go HTTP server  ←→  GossipSub pubsub  ←→  Peers
```

}\n    #status {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 8px;\n      font-size: .8rem;\n      color: var(--muted);\n    }\n    #dot {\n      width: 8px; height: 8px;\n      border-radius: 50%;\n      background: var(--danger);\n      transition: background .3s;\n    }\n    #dot.connected { background: var(--success); }\n\n    /* ── Peers pill ── */\n    #peers-badge {\n      background: var(--border);\n      border-radius: 20px;\n      padding: 2px 10px;\n      font-size: .75rem;\n      color: var(--muted);\n    }\n\n    /* ── Messages ── */\n    #messages {\n      flex: 1;\n      overflow-y: auto;\n      padding: 16px 20px;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      gap: 4px;\n      scroll-behavior: smooth;\n    }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 6px; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; }\n\n    .msg {\n      display: flex;\n      gap: 10px;\n      align-items: baseline;\n      padding: 3px 6px;\n      border-radius: 6px;\n      transition: background .15s;\n    }\n    .msg:hover { background: var(--surface); }\n    .msg.self .sender { color: var(--me); }\n    .msg.peer .sender { color: var(--peer); }\n    .msg.system .sender { color: var(--muted); font-style: italic; }\n    .timestamp {\n      font-size: .7rem;\n      color: var(--muted);\n      min-width: 38px;\n      flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    .sender {\n      font-weight: 600;\n      font-size: .85rem;\n      min-width: 80px;\n      flex-shrink: 0;\n      white-space: nowrap;\n      overflow: hidden;\n      text-overflow: ellipsis;\n    }\n    .body { font-size: .9rem; word-break: break-word; line-height: 1.4; }\n\n    /* ── History divider ── */\n    .divider {\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      gap: 8px;\n      color: var(--muted);\n      font-size: .72rem;\n      margin: 8px 0;\n    }\n    .divider::before, .divider::after {\n      content: '';\n      flex: 1;\n      height: 1px;\n      background: var(--border);\n    }\n\n    /* ── Input bar ── */\n    footer {\n      padding: 12px 20px;\n      background: var(--surface);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n      display: flex;\n      gap: 10px;\n      flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    #input {\n      flex: 1;\n      background: var(--bg);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: var(--radius);\n      color: var(--text);\n      font-size: .95rem;\n      padding: 10px 14px;\n      outline: none;\n      transition: border-color .2s;\n    }\n    #input:focus { border-color: var(--accent); }\n    #input::placeholder { color: var(--muted); }\n    #send {\n      background: var(--accent);\n      color: #fff;\n      border: none;\n      border-radius: var(--radius);\n      padding: 10px 20px;\n      font-size: .9rem;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      cursor: pointer;\n      transition: opacity .2s;\n    }\n    #send:hover { opacity: .85; }\n    #send:active { opacity: .7; }\n    #send:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n\n<header>\n  <h1>GrooveGO &nbsp;<span id=\"ws-name\">#general</span></h1>\n  <div id=\"status\">\n    <div id=\"dot\"></div>\n    <span id=\"conn-label\">connecting…</span>\n    <div id=\"peers-badge\">0 peers</div>\n  </div>\n</header>\n\n<div id=\"messages\"></div>\n\n<footer>\n  <input id=\"input\" type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Type a message and press Enter…\" autocomplete=\"off\" />\n  <button id=\"send\" disabled>Send</button>\n</footer>\n\n<script>\n  const messagesEl = document.getElementById('messages');\n  const inputEl    = document.getElementById('input');\n  const sendBtn    = document.getElementById('send');\n  const dot        = document.getElementById('dot');\n  const connLabel  = document.getElementById('conn-label');\n  const peersBadge = document.getElementById('peers-badge');\n  const wsName     = document.getElementById('ws-name');\n\n  let selfID = '';\n\n  // ── WebSocket ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const proto = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';\n  const ws    = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${location.host}/ws`);\n\n  ws.onopen = () => {\n    dot.classList.add('connected');\n    connLabel.textContent = 'connected';\n    sendBtn.disabled = false;\n    addSystem('Connected to node');\n  };\n\n  ws.onclose = () => {\n    dot.classList.remove('connected');\n    connLabel.textContent = 'disconnected';\n    sendBtn.disabled = true;\n    addSystem('Disconnected — refresh to reconnect');\n  };\n\n  ws.onerror = () => addSystem('WebSocket error');\n\n  ws.onmessage = (evt) => {\n    const env = JSON.parse(evt.data);\n\n    if (env.type === 'init') {\n      selfID = env.self_id;\n      wsName.textContent = '#' + env.workspace;\n      if (env.history && env.history.length > 0) {\n        addDivider(`${env.history.length} message(s) from previous session`);\n        env.history.forEach(m => addMessage(m, /*isHistory*/true));\n        addDivider('live');\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (env.type === 'peers') {\n      const n = env.count;\n      peersBadge.textContent = `${n} peer${n !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (env.type === 'message') {\n      addMessage(env.message, false);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function addMessage(m, isHistory) {\n    const isSelf = m.from === selfID;\n    const div    = document.createElement('div');\n    div.className = `msg ${isSelf ? 'self' : 'peer'}${isHistory ? ' history' : ''}`;\n\n    const ts   = new Date(m.ts);\n    const time = ts.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'});\n\n    div.innerHTML = `\n      <span class=\"timestamp\">${time}</span>\n      <span class=\"sender\">${isSelf ? 'me' : shortID(m.from)}</span>\n      <span class=\"body\">${escHtml(m.body)}</span>`;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(div);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addSystem(text) {\n    const div = document.createElement('div');\n    div.className = 'msg system';\n    div.innerHTML = `<span class=\"timestamp\"></span><span class=\"sender\">—</span><span class=\"body\">${escHtml(text)}</span>`;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(div);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addDivider(label) {\n    const div = document.createElement('div');\n    div.className = 'divider';\n    div.textContent = label;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(div);\n  }\n\n  function scrollBottom() {\n    messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;\n  }\n\n  function shortID(id) { return id.length > 12 ? id.slice(0, 12) : id; }\n\n  function escHtml(s) {\n    return s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')\n            .replace(/\"/g,'&quot;').replace(/'/g,'&#039;');\n  }\n\n  // ── Send ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function send() {\n    const text = inputEl.value.trim();\n    if (!text || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;\n    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'send', body: text }));\n    inputEl.value = '';\n  }\n\n  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', send);\n  inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') send(); });\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

opacity: .4; }\n\n    /* ── Input bar ── */\n    footer {\n      padding: 12px 20px;\n      background: var(--surface);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n      display: flex;\n      gap: 10px;\n      flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    #input {\n      flex: 1;\n      background: var(--surface2);\n      border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 10px;\n      color: var(--text);\n      font-size: .92rem;\n      padding: 10px 16px;\n      outline: none;\n      transition: border-color .2s, box-shadow .2s;\n    }\n    #input:focus {\n      border-color: var(--accent);\n      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, transparent);\n    }\n    #input::placeholder { color: var(--muted); }\n\n    #send {\n      background: var(--accent);\n      color: #fff;\n      border: none;\n      border-radius: 10px;\n      padding: 10px 22px;\n      font-size: .9rem;\n      font-weight: 600;\n      cursor: pointer;\n      transition: background .2s, transform .1s;\n      white-space: nowrap;\n    }\n    #send:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--accent-h); }\n    #send:active:not(:disabled) { transform: scale(.97); }\n    #send:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n\n<header>\n  <div class=\"logo\">\n    <div class=\"logo-icon\">🎵</div>\n    <span class=\"logo-text\">Groove<span>GO</span></span>\n  </div>\n  <div id=\"ws-name\">#general</div>\n  <div class=\"header-right\">\n    <div id=\"peers-badge\">0 peers</div>\n    <div id=\"status-dot\"></div>\n    <button id=\"theme-btn\" title=\"Toggle light/dark\">🌙</button>\n  </div>\n</header>\n\n<div id=\"messages\">\n  <div id=\"empty\">\n    <div class=\"icon\">💬</div>\n    <div>No messages yet — say hello!</div>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<footer>\n  <input id=\"input\" type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Type a message…\" autocomplete=\"off\" />\n  <button id=\"send\" disabled>Send</button>\n</footer>\n\n<script>\n  const messagesEl = document.getElementById('messages');\n  const emptyEl    = document.getElementById('empty');\n  const inputEl    = document.getElementById('input');\n  const sendBtn    = document.getElementById('send');\n  const dot        = document.getElementById('status-dot');\n  const peersBadge = document.getElementById('peers-badge');\n  const wsNameEl   = document.getElementById('ws-name');\n  const themeBtn   = document.getElementById('theme-btn');\n  const html       = document.documentElement;\n\n  let selfID = '';\n  let msgCount = 0;\n\n  // ── Theme ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('groove-theme') || 'dark';\n  setTheme(savedTheme);\n\n  themeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {\n    setTheme(html.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark');\n  });\n\n  function setTheme(t) {\n    html.dataset.theme = t;\n    themeBtn.textContent = t === 'dark' ? '☀️' : '🌙';\n    localStorage.setItem('groove-theme', t);\n  }\n\n  // ── WebSocket ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const proto = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';\n  const ws    = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${location.host}/ws`);\n\n  ws.onopen = () => {\n    dot.classList.add('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = false;\n    addSystem('Connected to node');\n  };\n\n  ws.onclose = () => {\n    dot.classList.remove('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = true;\n    addSystem('Disconnected — refresh to reconnect');\n  };\n\n  ws.onerror = () => addSystem('WebSocket error');\n\n  ws.onmessage = (evt) => {\n    const env = JSON.parse(evt.data);\n\n    if (env.type === 'init') {\n      selfID = env.self_id;\n      wsNameEl.textContent = '#' + env.workspace;\n      if (env.history && env.history.length > 0) {\n        addDivider(`${env.history.length} previous message${env.history.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`);\n        env.history.forEach(m => addMessage(m));\n        addDivider('live');\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (env.type === 'peers') {\n      const n = env.count;\n      peersBadge.textContent = `${n} peer${n !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;\n      peersBadge.classList.toggle('has-peers', n > 0);\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (env.type === 'message') {\n      addMessage(env.message);\n    }\n  };\n\n  // ── Render helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function addMessage(m) {\n    if (msgCount === 0) emptyEl.remove();\n    msgCount++;\n\n    const isSelf = m.from === selfID;\n    const row    = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = `msg ${isSelf ? 'self' : 'peer'}`;\n\n    const ts   = new Date(m.ts);\n    const time = ts.toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });\n\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\">${time}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">${isSelf ? 'me' : shortID(m.from)}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${escHtml(m.body)}</span>`;\n\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addSystem(text) {\n    const row = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = 'msg system';\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\"></span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">—</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${escHtml(text)}</span>`;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addDivider(label) {\n    const div = document.createElement('div');\n    div.className = 'divider';\n    div.textContent = label;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(div);\n  }\n\n  function scrollBottom() {\n    messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight;\n  }\n\n  function shortID(id) { return id.length > 12 ? id.slice(0, 12) : id; }\n\n  function escHtml(s) {\n    return s.replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')\n            .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;');\n  }\n\n  // ── Send ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function send() {\n    const text = inputEl.value.trim();\n    if (!text || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;\n    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'send', body: text }));\n    inputEl.value = '';\n  }\n\n  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', send);\n  inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') send(); });\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/web/server.go",
  "content": "package web\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"embed\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io/fs\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/gorilla/websocket\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n)\n\n//go:embed static\nvar staticFiles embed.FS\n\n// Envelope types sent over WebSocket to the browser.\ntype initEnv struct {\n\tType      string          `json:\"type\"`      // \"init\"\n\tSelfID    string          `json:\"self_id\"`\n\tWorkspace string          `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tHistory   []store.Message `json:\"history\"`\n}\n\ntype msgEnv struct {\n\tType    string          `json:\"type\"` // \"message\"\n\tMessage store.Message   `json:\"message\"`\n}\n\ntype peersEnv struct {\n\tType  string `json:\"type\"` // \"peers\"\n\tCount int    `json:\"count\"`\n}\n\n// clientSend is what the browser sends to us.\ntype clientSend struct {\n\tType string `json:\"type\"` // \"send\"\n\tBody string `json:\"body\"`\n}\n\nvar upgrader = websocket.Upgrader{\n\tCheckOrigin: func(r *http.Request) bool { return true }, // allow all origins for local use\n}\n\n// Server serves the GrooveGO web UI and bridges WebSocket clients to pubsub.\ntype Server struct {\n\tselfID    string\n\tworkspace string\n\tws        *transport.Workspace\n\tstore     *store.Store\n\tgetPeers  func() int\n}\n\n// New creates a Server. Call ListenAndServe to start it.\nfunc New(selfID, workspace string, ws *transport.Workspace, s *store.Store, getPeers func() int) *Server {\n\treturn &Server{\n\t\tselfID:    selfID,\n\t\tworkspace: workspace,\n\t\tws:        ws,\n\t\tstore:     s,\n\t\tgetPeers:  getPeers,\n\t}\n}\n\n// ListenAndServe starts the HTTP server on addr (e.g. \":8080\").\nfunc (srv *Server) ListenAndServe(ctx context.Context, addr string) error {\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\n\t// Serve static files from embedded FS\n\tsub, err := fs.Sub(staticFiles, \"static\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tmux.Handle(\"/\", http.FileServer(http.FS(sub)))\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/ws\", srv.handleWS)\n\n\thttpSrv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: mux}\n\n\tgo func() {\n\t\t<-ctx.Done()\n\t\t_ = httpSrv.Close()\n\t}()\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[web] UI available at http://localhost%s\\n\", addr)\n\tif err := httpSrv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\nfunc (srv *Server) handleWS(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tconn, err := upgrader.Upgrade(w, r, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(r.Context())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Send init frame with history\n\thistory, _ := srv.store.History(srv.workspace)\n\tif history == nil {\n\t\thistory = []store.Message{}\n\t}\n\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, initEnv{\n\t\tType:      \"init\",\n\t\tSelfID:    srv.selfID,\n\t\tWorkspace: srv.workspace,\n\t\tHistory:   history,\n\t})\n\n\t// Pump incoming pubsub messages → browser\n\tincoming := make(chan transport.Message, 32)\n\tgo srv.ws.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(m transport.Message) {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase incoming <- m:\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\t// Periodic peer count\n\tpeerTick := time.NewTicker(2 * time.Second)\n\tdefer peerTick.Stop()\n\n\t// Read from browser in separate goroutine\n\tbrowserMsgs := make(chan clientSend, 8)\n\tbrowserDone := make(chan struct{})\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer close(browserDone)\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t_, data, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcancel()\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar cm clientSend\n\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(data, &cm); err == nil && cm.Type == \"send\" && cm.Body != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tbrowserMsgs <- cm\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase <-browserDone:\n\t\t\treturn\n\n\t\tcase cm := <-browserMsgs:\n\t\t\tif err := srv.ws.Publish(ctx, cm.Body); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t// Echo back to this browser client as a regular message\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, msgEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType: \"message\",\n\t\t\t\t\tMessage: store.Message{\n\t\t\t\t\t\tFrom:      srv.selfID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tWorkspace: srv.workspace,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tBody:      cm.Body,\n\t\t\t\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tcase m := <-incoming:\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, msgEnv{\n\t\t\t\tType:    \"message\",\n\t\t\t\tMessage: store.Message(m),\n\t\t\t})\n\n\t\tcase <-peerTick.C:\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, peersEnv{Type: \"peers\", Count: srv.getPeers()})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (srv *Server) writeJSON(conn *websocket.Conn, v any) {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(v)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t_ = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, data)\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/cmd/groove-web/main.go",
  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/web\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"libp2p TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\thttpAddr  := flag.String(\"http\", \":8080\", \"Web UI listen address\")\n\tworkspace := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Workspace name to join\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Directory for local message store\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Local persistence\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(*dataDir, *workspace))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer s.Close()\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// GossipSub router\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Join workspace topic\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace, s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tgetPeers := func() int { return len(ws.ListPeers()) }\n\n\t// Start web server\n\twebSrv := web.New(n.ID.String(), *workspace, ws, s, getPeers)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tif err := webSrv.ListenAndServe(ctx, *httpAddr); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[web] %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove-web] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}
