> AGENT

Pushed `feb4800`. Here's the full picture:

**To run the web UI:**
```bash
cd groove-go
go run ./cmd/groove-web --workspace general
# open http://localhost:8080 in any browser
```

**What you get:**

| Feature | Detail |
|---------|--------|
| Dark theme | Deep navy bg, purple accents, clean grid layout |
| Light theme | Clean white, same purple accents |
| Theme toggle | ☀️/🌙 button, persists across refreshes |
| Peer count | Green pill in header, updates every 2s |
| Message history | Loaded from Badger on connect, shown above a "live" divider |
| Real-time | WebSocket push, no polling |
| No build step | HTML is `embed`ded in the binary |

**Three binaries available:**

| Binary | Use |
|--------|-----|
| `groove` | Headless CLI |
| `groove-ui` | Bubble Tea terminal UI |
| `groove-web` | Browser UI at `localhost:8080` |

Ready for Phase 4 (CRDT documents) or want to explore something else first?

> DEVELOPER

How to display two nodes?

> AGENT

Run two `groove-web` instances on different ports — they'll find each other automatically via mDNS:

**Terminal 1:**
```bash
cd groove-go
go run ./cmd/groove-web --port 9000 --http :8080 --workspace general
```
Open **http://localhost:8080**

**Terminal 2:**
```bash
cd groove-go
go run ./cmd/groove-web --port 9001 --http :8081 --workspace general
```
Open **http://localhost:8081**

Within a few seconds both nodes discover each other via mDNS. The peer count badge turns green and shows **1 peer**. Type a message in one browser tab — it appears in the other.

---

**To simulate two separate machines**, run one instance per machine on the same LAN. Same commands, just omit `--port` (random) and use `--http :8080` on both. mDNS handles discovery automatically.

**To use a custom workspace** (separate chat rooms):
```bash
--workspace safecast   # only peers in the same workspace see each other's messages
```

> DEVELOPER

same url?

> AGENT

No — each node runs its own HTTP server, so they need different ports (`8080` and `8081`). You open **two browser tabs** and tile them side by side.

The simplest way to see both at once:

1. Open `http://localhost:8080` in one tab
2. Open `http://localhost:8081` in a second tab  
3. Snap them side by side (or use a split-screen extension)

---

If you want **a single URL showing both nodes**, I can build a "monitor" page — a third server that connects to both nodes via WebSocket and renders both chat streams in a split view:

```
http://localhost:9000
┌─────────────────┬─────────────────┐
│   Node A        │   Node B        │
│  #general       │  #general       │
│                 │                 │
│  12:03 me: hi   │  12:03 QmXf: hi │
│                 │                 │
│  [input]  Send  │  [input]  Send  │
└─────────────────┴─────────────────┘
```

> DEVELOPER

rob@rob-GS66-Stealth-10UG:~/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go$ cd groove-go
go run ./cmd/groove-web --port 9001 --http :8081 --workspace general
bash: cd: groove-go: No such file or directory
fatal: store open: Cannot acquire directory lock on "/home/rob/.groove/general".  Another process is using this Badger database. err: resource temporarily unavailable
exit status 1

> AGENT

Two issues:

**1. Directory** — you're already inside `groove-go`, don't `cd` again.

**2. Badger lock** — both nodes are trying to use the same database at `~/.groove/general`. Each node needs its own data directory.

Run them like this:

**Terminal 1** (already running, leave it):
```bash
go run ./cmd/groove-web --port 9000 --http :8080 --workspace general
```

**Terminal 2:**
```bash
go run ./cmd/groove-web --port 9001 --http :8081 --workspace general --data ~/.groove-node2
```

The `--data` flag gives node 2 its own isolated store. That's all that's needed.

---

I should also make the default data dir smarter so this doesn't bite you again. Want me to fix it to auto-suffix the port number (e.g. `~/.groove/node-9001`) when no `--data` is provided?

> DEVELOPER

can we send files too?

> AGENT

Yes! Good feature to add. Here's the plan:

**Approach:**
- Small files (< ~1MB) — encode as base64, send over the existing GossipSub pubsub topic alongside chat messages
- Large files — direct libp2p stream between peers (faster, no size limit, no relay overhead)

For now the most useful/practical is a **hybrid**:

| File size | Method |
|-----------|--------|
| < 512 KB | Base64 over pubsub (simple, works immediately) |
| ≥ 512 KB | Direct libp2p stream (peer-to-peer, fast) |

**What it looks like in the browser UI:**
```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ GrooveGO  #general          1 peer  ☀️  │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ 12:03  me:       hello                  │
│ 12:04  QmXf3r:   hi!                   │
│ 12:05  me:       📎 photo.jpg (240 KB)  │
│ 12:05  QmXf3r:   [Download photo.jpg]  │
├─────────────────────────────────────────┤
│ [Type a message…]  📎  [Send]           │
└─────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Changes needed:**
1. `internal/transport/pubsub.go` — add `FileMessage` type, publish/receive file chunks
2. `internal/store/store.go` — save received files to `~/.groove/files/`
3. `internal/web/server.go` — handle file upload from browser, serve download endpoint
4. `internal/web/static/index.html` — paperclip button, drag-and-drop, download link

1	package transport
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"encoding/json"
6		"fmt"
7		"time"
8	
9		pubsub "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub"
10		"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host"
11		"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer"
12		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store"
13	)
14	
15	// Message is the wire format for all workspace messages.
16	type Message struct {
17		From      string    `json:"from"`
18		Workspace string    `json:"workspace"`
19		Body      string    `json:"body"`
20		Timestamp time.Time `json:"ts"`
21	}
22	
23	// Workspace represents a joined pubsub topic (a shared channel).
24	type Workspace struct {
25		name  string
26		self  peer.ID
27		topic *pubsub.Topic
28		sub   *pubsub.Subscription
29		ps    *pubsub.PubSub
30		store *store.Store
31	}
32	
33	// NewGossipSub creates a GossipSub router attached to the given host.
34	func NewGossipSub(ctx context.Context, h host.Host) (*pubsub.PubSub, error) {
35		ps, err := pubsub.NewGossipSub(ctx, h)
36		if err != nil {
37			return nil, fmt.Errorf("gossipsub: %w", err)
38		}
39		fmt.Println("[transport] GossipSub router started")
40		return ps, nil
41	}
42	
43	// JoinWorkspace joins (or creates) a pubsub topic for the named workspace.
44	// Pass a non-nil store to enable persistence.
45	func JoinWorkspace(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, name string, s *store.Store) (*Workspace, error) {
46		topic, err := ps.Join("workspace-" + name)
47		if err != nil {
48			return nil, fmt.Errorf("join topic: %w", err)
49		}
50	
51		sub, err := topic.Subscribe()
52		if err != nil {
53			return nil, fmt.Errorf("subscribe: %w", err)
54		}
55	
56		fmt.Printf("[transport] joined workspace: %s\n", name)
57		return &Workspace{
58			name:  name,
59			self:  self,
60			topic: topic,
61			sub:   sub,
62			ps:    ps,
63			store: s,
64		}, nil
65	}
66	
67	// Publish sends a message to the workspace topic and persists it locally.
68	func (w *Workspace) Publish(ctx context.Context, body string) error {
69		msg := Message{
70			From:      w.self.String(),
71			Workspace: w.name,
72			Body:      body,
73			Timestamp: time.Now().UTC(),
74		}
75		data, err := json.Marshal(msg)
76		if err != nil {
77			return err
78		}
79		if err := w.topic.Publish(ctx, data); err != nil {
80			return err
81		}
82		if w.store != nil {
83			_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))
84		}
85		return nil
86	}
87	
88	// ReadLoop blocks and prints every incoming message, persisting each one.
89	// Returns when ctx is cancelled.
90	func (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {
91		w.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(msg Message) {
92			fmt.Printf("[%s] %s: %s\n", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)
93		})
94	}
95	
96	// ReadLoopInto blocks and calls fn for every incoming message from other peers.
97	// Messages are also persisted to the store if one is set.
98	// Returns when ctx is cancelled.
99	func (w *Workspace) ReadLoopInto(ctx context.Context, fn func(Message)) {
100		for {
101			m, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)
102			if err != nil {
103				return
104			}
105			// Skip our own messages.
106			if m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {
107				continue
108			}
109			var msg Message
110			if err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {
111				continue
112			}
113			if w.store != nil {
114				_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))
115			}
116			fn(msg)
117		}
118	}
119	
120	// ListPeers returns the peer IDs currently subscribed to this workspace topic.
121	func (w *Workspace) ListPeers() []peer.ID {
122		return w.ps.ListPeers("workspace-" + w.name)
123	}
124	
125	func shortID(id string) string {
126		if len(id) > 12 {
127			return id[:12]
128		}
129		return id
130	}
131

\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\tMsgTypeChat = \"chat\"\n\tMsgTypeFile = \"file\"\n\tMaxFileSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024 // 10 MB\n)\n\n// Message is the wire format for all workspace messages (chat and file).\ntype Message struct {\n\tFrom      string    `json:\"from\"`\n\tWorkspace string    `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tTimestamp time.Time `json:\"ts\"`\n\t// File fields (only set when MsgType == \"file\")\n\tMsgType  string `json:\"msg_type,omitempty\"`\n\tFileID   string `json:\"file_id,omitempty\"`\n\tFileName string `json:\"file_name,omitempty\"`\n\tFileMime string `json:\"file_mime,omitempty\"`\n\tFileSize int64  `json:\"file_size,omitempty\"`\n\tFileData string `json:\"file_data,omitempty\"` // base64-encoded bytes\n}\n\n// Workspace represents a joined pubsub topic (a shared channel).\ntype Workspace struct {\n\tname  string\n\tself  peer.ID\n\ttopic *pubsub.Topic\n\tsub   *pubsub.Subscription\n\tps    *pubsub.PubSub\n\tstore *store.Store\n}\n\n// NewGossipSub creates a GossipSub router with a 16 MB max message size.\nfunc NewGossipSub(ctx context.Context, h host.Host) (*pubsub.PubSub, error) {\n\tps, err := pubsub.NewGossipSub(ctx, h,\n\t\tpubsub.WithMaxMessageSize(16*1024*1024),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"gossipsub: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Println(\"[transport] GossipSub router started\")\n\treturn ps, nil\n}\n\n// JoinWorkspace joins (or creates) a pubsub topic for the named workspace.\nfunc JoinWorkspace(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, name string, s *store.Store) (*Workspace, error) {\n\ttopic, err := ps.Join(\"workspace-\" + name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"join topic: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tsub, err := topic.Subscribe()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"subscribe: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[transport] joined workspace: %s\\n\", name)\n\treturn &Workspace{name: name, self: self, topic: topic, sub: sub, ps: ps, store: s}, nil\n}\n\n// Publish sends a chat message to the workspace topic and persists it locally.\nfunc (w *Workspace) Publish(ctx context.Context, body string) error {\n\tmsg := Message{\n\t\tMsgType:   MsgTypeChat,\n\t\tFrom:      w.self.String(),\n\t\tWorkspace: w.name,\n\t\tBody:      body,\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t}\n\treturn w.publish(ctx, msg)\n}\n\n// PublishFile sends a file to the workspace topic (max 10 MB).\nfunc (w *Workspace) PublishFile(ctx context.Context, fileID, fileName, mime string, data []byte) error {\n\tif len(data) > MaxFileSize {\n\t\treturn fmt.Errorf(\"file too large (%d bytes, max %d)\", len(data), MaxFileSize)\n\t}\n\tmsg := Message{\n\t\tMsgType:   MsgTypeFile,\n\t\tFrom:      w.self.String(),\n\t\tWorkspace: w.name,\n\t\tBody:      fmt.Sprintf(\"📎 %s (%s)\", fileName, humanSize(int64(len(data)))),\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\tFileID:    fileID,\n\t\tFileName:  fileName,\n\t\tFileMime:  mime,\n\t\tFileSize:  int64(len(data)),\n\t\tFileData:  base64.StdEncoding.EncodeToString(data),\n\t}\n\treturn w.publish(ctx, msg)\n}\n\nfunc (w *Workspace) publish(ctx context.Context, msg Message) error {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(msg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif err := w.topic.Publish(ctx, data); err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(msg))\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// ReadLoop blocks and prints every incoming message.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tw.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(msg Message) {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[%s] %s: %s\\n\", msg.Workspace, shortID(msg.From), msg.Body)\n\t})\n}\n\n// ReadLoopInto blocks and calls fn for every incoming message from other peers.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ReadLoopInto(ctx context.Context, fn func(Message)) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := w.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tif m.ReceivedFrom == w.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar msg Message\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &msg); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tif w.store != nil {\n\t\t\t// Save metadata only (strip binary payload before storing)\n\t\t\tmeta := msg\n\t\t\tmeta.FileData = \"\"\n\t\t\t_ = w.store.Save(store.Message(meta))\n\t\t}\n\t\tfn(msg)\n\t}\n}\n\n// ListPeers returns the peer IDs currently in this workspace topic.\nfunc (w *Workspace) ListPeers() []peer.ID {\n\treturn w.ps.ListPeers(\"workspace-\" + w.name)\n}\n\nfunc shortID(id string) string {\n\tif len(id) > 12 {\n\t\treturn id[:12]\n\t}\n\treturn id\n}\n\nfunc humanSize(b int64) string {\n\tswitch {\n\tcase b >= 1024*1024:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%.1f MB\", float64(b)/1024/1024)\n\tcase b >= 1024:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%.1f KB\", float64(b)/1024)\n\tdefault:\n\t\treturn fmt.Sprintf(\"%d B\", b)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

1	package store
2	
3	import (
4		"encoding/json"
5		"fmt"
6		"time"
7	
8		badger "github.com/dgraph-io/badger/v4"
9	)
10	
11	// Store wraps a Badger key-value database for local message persistence.
12	type Store struct {
13		db *badger.DB
14	}
15	
16	// Message mirrors transport.Message — duplicated here to avoid a circular dep.
17	type Message struct {
18		From      string    `json:"from"`
19		Workspace string    `json:"workspace"`
20		Body      string    `json:"body"`
21		Timestamp time.Time `json:"ts"`
22	}
23	
24	// Open opens (or creates) a Badger database at the given directory path.
25	func Open(dir string) (*Store, error) {
26		opts := badger.DefaultOptions(dir).WithLogger(nil)
27		db, err := badger.Open(opts)
28		if err != nil {
29			return nil, fmt.Errorf("store open: %w", err)
30		}
31		fmt.Printf("[store] opened at %s\n", dir)
32		return &Store{db: db}, nil
33	}
34	
35	// Close flushes and closes the database.
36	func (s *Store) Close() error {
37		return s.db.Close()
38	}
39	
40	// Save persists a message. Key format: msg/<workspace>/<unix-nano>/<from>
41	func (s *Store) Save(msg Message) error {
42		key := fmt.Sprintf("msg/%s/%d/%s", msg.Workspace, msg.Timestamp.UnixNano(), msg.From)
43		val, err := json.Marshal(msg)
44		if err != nil {
45			return err
46		}
47		return s.db.Update(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {
48			return txn.Set([]byte(key), val)
49		})
50	}
51	
52	// History returns all stored messages for a workspace, oldest first.
53	func (s *Store) History(workspace string) ([]Message, error) {
54		prefix := []byte("msg/" + workspace + "/")
55		var msgs []Message
56	
57		err := s.db.View(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {
58			opts := badger.DefaultIteratorOptions
59			opts.Prefix = prefix
60			it := txn.NewIterator(opts)
61			defer it.Close()
62	
63			for it.Rewind(); it.Valid(); it.Next() {
64				item := it.Item()
65				if err := item.Value(func(v []byte) error {
66					var m Message
67					if err := json.Unmarshal(v, &m); err != nil {
68						return err
69					}
70					msgs = append(msgs, m)
71					return nil
72				}); err != nil {
73					return err
74				}
75			}
76			return nil
77		})
78		return msgs, err
79	}
80

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/store/store.go",
  "content": "package store\n\nimport (\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\tbadger \"github.com/dgraph-io/badger/v4\"\n)\n\n// Store wraps a Badger key-value database for local message persistence.\ntype Store struct {\n\tdb       *badger.DB\n\tfilesDir string\n}\n\n// Message mirrors transport.Message — duplicated here to avoid a circular dep.\n// Fields must stay in sync with transport.Message.\ntype Message struct {\n\tFrom      string    `json:\"from\"`\n\tWorkspace string    `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tBody      string    `json:\"body\"`\n\tTimestamp time.Time `json:\"ts\"`\n\t// File fields (omitted when empty)\n\tMsgType  string `json:\"msg_type,omitempty\"`\n\tFileID   string `json:\"file_id,omitempty\"`\n\tFileName string `json:\"file_name,omitempty\"`\n\tFileMime string `json:\"file_mime,omitempty\"`\n\tFileSize int64  `json:\"file_size,omitempty\"`\n\tFileData string `json:\"file_data,omitempty\"`\n}\n\n// Open opens (or creates) a Badger database at the given directory path.\nfunc Open(dir string) (*Store, error) {\n\topts := badger.DefaultOptions(dir).WithLogger(nil)\n\tdb, err := badger.Open(opts)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"store open: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfilesDir := filepath.Join(dir, \"files\")\n\tif err := os.MkdirAll(filesDir, 0755); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"store files dir: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[store] opened at %s\\n\", dir)\n\treturn &Store{db: db, filesDir: filesDir}, nil\n}\n\n// Close flushes and closes the database.\nfunc (s *Store) Close() error {\n\treturn s.db.Close()\n}\n\n// Save persists a message (metadata only — FileData should be stripped before calling).\n// Key format: msg/<workspace>/<unix-nano>/<from>\nfunc (s *Store) Save(msg Message) error {\n\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"msg/%s/%d/%s\", msg.Workspace, msg.Timestamp.UnixNano(), msg.From)\n\tval, err := json.Marshal(msg)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn s.db.Update(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {\n\t\treturn txn.Set([]byte(key), val)\n\t})\n}\n\n// History returns all stored messages for a workspace, oldest first.\nfunc (s *Store) History(workspace string) ([]Message, error) {\n\tprefix := []byte(\"msg/\" + workspace + \"/\")\n\tvar msgs []Message\n\n\terr := s.db.View(func(txn *badger.Txn) error {\n\t\topts := badger.DefaultIteratorOptions\n\t\topts.Prefix = prefix\n\t\tit := txn.NewIterator(opts)\n\t\tdefer it.Close()\n\n\t\tfor it.Rewind(); it.Valid(); it.Next() {\n\t\t\titem := it.Item()\n\t\t\tif err := item.Value(func(v []byte) error {\n\t\t\t\tvar m Message\n\t\t\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(v, &m); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tmsgs = append(msgs, m)\n\t\t\t\treturn nil\n\t\t\t}); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn err\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn nil\n\t})\n\treturn msgs, err\n}\n\n// SaveFile writes raw file bytes to the files directory.\n// Returns the path the file was saved to.\nfunc (s *Store) SaveFile(fileID, fileName string, data []byte) (string, error) {\n\t// Sanitise filename — keep only the base name\n\tsafe := filepath.Base(fileName)\n\tpath := filepath.Join(s.filesDir, fileID+\"_\"+safe)\n\tif err := os.WriteFile(path, data, 0644); err != nil {\n\t\treturn \"\", fmt.Errorf(\"save file: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\treturn path, nil\n}\n\n// FilePath returns the on-disk path for a previously saved file.\nfunc (s *Store) FilePath(fileID, fileName string) string {\n\tsafe := filepath.Base(fileName)\n\treturn filepath.Join(s.filesDir, fileID+\"_\"+safe)\n}\n\n// FilesDir returns the directory where received files are stored.\nfunc (s *Store) FilesDir() string {\n\treturn s.filesDir\n}\n"
}

 string `json:\"type\"`\n\tMessage string `json:\"message\"`\n}\n\n// clientSend is what the browser sends us over WebSocket.\ntype clientSend struct {\n\tType string `json:\"type\"` // \"send\"\n\tBody string `json:\"body\"`\n}\n\nvar upgrader = websocket.Upgrader{\n\tCheckOrigin: func(r *http.Request) bool { return true },\n}\n\n// Server serves the GrooveGO web UI and bridges getPeers: getPeers}\n}\n\n// ListenAndServe starts the HTTP server on addr (e.g. \":8080\").\nfunc (srv *Server) ListenAndServe(ctx context.Context, addr string) error {\n\tmux := {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleUpload accepts a multipart file POST, publishes it over pubsub, saves locally.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleUpload(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := r.ParseMultipartForm(maxUploadSize); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"file too large (max 10 MB)\", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfile, header, err := r.FormFile(\"file\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"missing {\n\t\tmimeType = \"application/octet-stream\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Save locally\n\tif _, err := srv.store.SaveFile(fileID, header.Filename, data); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"storage error\", r.URL.Path[len(\"/files/\"):]\n\t// split into fileID / fileName\n\tslash := len(rest)\n\tfor i, c := range rest {\n\t\tif c == '/' {\n\t\t\tslash = i\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif slash >= len(rest) {\n\t\thttp.NotFound(w, r)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfileID  := rest[:slash]\n\tfileName := rest[slash+1:]\n\tpath := srv.store.FilePath(fileID, fileName)\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\", fmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename=\"%s\"`, filepath.Base(fileName)))\n\thttp.ServeFile(w, r, path)\n}\n\n// handleWS upgrades the connection and bridges pubsub ↔ browser.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleWS(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tconn, err := upgrader.Upgrade(w, r, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\tctx, cancel := transport.Message, 32)\n\tgo srv.ws.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(m transport.Message) {\n\t\t// Save file to disk when received\n\t\tif m.MsgType == transport.MsgTypeFile && m.FileData != \"\" {\n\t\t\tif m:\n\t\tdefault:\n\t\t}\n\t})\n\n\tpeerTick := time.NewTicker(2 * time.Second)\n\tdefer peerTick.Stop()\n\n\tbrowserMsgs := make(chan clientSend, 8)\n\tbrowserDone := make(chan struct{})\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer close(browserDone)\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t_, data, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcancel()\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar cm clientSend\n\t\t\tif err := conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, data)\n}\n"

dashed var(--accent);\n      align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      font-size: 1.4rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--accent);\n      pointer-events: none;\n    }\n    body.dragging #drop-overlay { display: flex; }\n\n    /* ── Messages ── */\n    #messages {\n      flex: 1; overflow-y: auto;\n      padding: 20px; display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;\n    }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 5px; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; }\n\n    .msg {\n      display: grid;\n      grid-template-columns: 42px 90px 1fr;\n      align-items: baseline; gap: 0 8px;\n      padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 8px; transition: background .12s;\n    }\n    .msg:hover { background: var(--surface2); }\n    .msg.self   .sender { color: var(--me); }\n    .msg.peer   .sender { color: var(--peer); }\n    .msg.system .sender { color: var(--system); font-style: italic; }\n    .timestamp { font-size: .7rem; color: var(--muted); text-align: right; white-space: nowrap; }\n    .sender { font-weight: 600; font-size: .82rem; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }\n    .body { font-size: .9rem; word-break: break-word; line-height: 1.5; }\n\n    /* ── File bubble ── */\n    .file-bubble {\n      display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      background: var(--file-bg); border: 1px solid var(--file-border);\n      border-radius: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; margin-top: 2px;\n      max-width: 320px;\n    }\n    .file-icon { font-size: 1.4rem; flex-shrink: 0; }\n    .file-info { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }\n    .file-name {\n      font-size: .85rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text);\n      white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis;\n    }\n    .file-meta { font-size: .72rem; color: var(--muted); }\n    .file-dl {\n      margin-left: auto; flex-shrink: 0;\n      background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none;\n      border-radius: 8px; padding: 5px 12px; font-size: .8rem;\n      font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; text-decoration: none;\n      transition: opacity .2s;\n    }\n    .file-dl:hover { opacity: .85; }\n\n    /* ── Divider ── */\n    .divider {\n      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      color: var(--muted); font-size: .7rem;\n      text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .06em; margin: 10px 8px;\n    }\n    .divider::before, .divider::after { content: ''; flex: 1; height: 1px; background: var(--border); }\n\n    /* ── Empty state ── */\n    #empty {\n      flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column;\n      align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      color: var(--muted); gap: 8px; font-size: .9rem; user-select: none;\n    }\n    #empty .icon { font-size: 2.5rem; opacity: .4; }\n\n    /* ── Upload progress ── */\n    #upload-bar {\n      display: none; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      padding: 6px 20px; background: var(--surface2);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border); font-size: .8rem; color: var(--muted);\n    }\n    #upload-bar.active { display: flex; }\n    #upload-progress { flex: 1; height: 4px; border-radius: 2px; accent-color: var(--accent); }\n\n    /* ── Input bar ── */\n    footer {\n      padding: 12px 20px; background: var(--surface);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n      display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-shrink: 0; align-items: center;\n    }\n    #attach-btn {\n      background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 10px; color: var(--muted); cursor: pointer;\n      font-size: 1.1rem; width: 42px; height: 42px;\n      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      transition: color .2s, border-color .2s; flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    #attach-btn:hover { color: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); }\n    #file-input { display: none; }\n    #input {\n      flex: 1; background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 10px; color: var(--text); font-size: .92rem;\n      padding: 10px 16px; outline: none;\n      transition: border-color .2s, box-shadow .2s;\n    }\n    #input:focus {\n      border-color: var(--accent);\n      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, transparent);\n    }\n    #input::placeholder { color: var(--muted); }\n    #send {\n      background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none;\n      border-radius: 10px; padding: 10px 22px; font-size: .9rem;\n      font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;\n      transition: background .2s, transform .1s;\n    }\n    #send:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--accent-h); }\n    #send:active:not(:disabled) { transform: scale(.97); }\n    #send:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n\n<div id=\"drop-overlay\">📎 Drop file to send</div>\n\n<header>\n  <div class=\"logo\">\n    <div class=\"logo-icon\">🎵</div>\n    <span class=\"logo-text\">Groove<span>GO</span></span>\n  </div>\n  <div id=\"ws-name\">#general</div>\n  <div class=\"header-right\">\n    <div id=\"peers-badge\">0 peers</div>\n    <div id=\"status-dot\"></div>\n    <button id=\"theme-btn\" title=\"Toggle light/dark\">🌙</button>\n  </div>\n</header>\n\n<div id=\"upload-bar\">\n  <span id=\"upload-label\">Uploading…</span>\n  <progress id=\"upload-progress\" max=\"100\" value=\"0\"></progress>\n</div>\n\n<div id=\"messages\">\n  <div id=\"empty\">\n    <div class=\"icon\">💬</div>\n    <div>No messages yet — say hello or drop a file!</div>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<footer>\n  <button id=\"attach-btn\" title=\"Send a file\">📎</button>\n  <input id=\"file-input\" type=\"file\" />\n  <input id=\"input\" type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Type a message…\" autocomplete=\"off\" />\n  <button id=\"send\" disabled>Send</button>\n</footer>\n\n<script>\n  const messagesEl  = document.getElementById('messages');\n  const emptyEl     = document.getElementById('empty');\n  const inputEl     = document.getElementById('input');\n  const sendBtn     = document.getElementById('send');\n  const dot         = document.getElementById('status-dot');\n  const peersBadge  = document.getElementById('peers-badge');\n  const wsNameEl    = document.getElementById('ws-name');\n  const themeBtn    = document.getElementById('theme-btn');\n  const html        = document.documentElement;\n  const attachBtn   = document.getElementById('attach-btn');\n  const fileInput   = document.getElementById('file-input');\n  const uploadBar   = document.getElementById('upload-bar');\n  const uploadLabel = document.getElementById('upload-label');\n  const uploadProg  = document.getElementById('upload-progress');\n\n  let selfID = '';\n  let msgCount = 0;\n\n  // ── Theme ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  setTheme(localStorage.getItem('groove-theme') || 'dark');\n  themeBtn.addEventListener('click', () =>\n    setTheme(html.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));\n\n  function setTheme(t) {\n    html.dataset.theme = t;\n    themeBtn.textContent = t === 'dark' ? '☀️' : '🌙';\n    localStorage.setItem('groove-theme', t);\n  }\n\n  // ── WebSocket ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const proto = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';\n  const ws    = new WebSocket(`${proto}://${location.host}/ws`);\n\n  ws.onopen = () => {\n    dot.classList.add('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = false;\n    addSystem('Connected to node');\n  };\n  ws.onclose = () => {\n    dot.classList.remove('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = true;\n    addSystem('Disconnected — refresh to reconnect');\n  };\n  ws.onerror = () => addSystem('WebSocket error');\n\n  ws.onmessage = (evt) => {\n    const env = JSON.parse(evt.data);\n    if (env.type === 'init') {\n      selfID = env.self_id;\n      wsNameEl.textContent = '#' + env.workspace;\n      if (env.history && env.history.length > 0) {\n        addDivider(`${env.history.length} previous message${env.history.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`);\n        env.history.forEach(m => addMessage(m));\n        addDivider('live');\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (env.type === 'peers') {\n      const n = env.count;\n      peersBadge.textContent = `${n} peer${n !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;\n      peersBadge.classList.toggle('has-peers', n > 0);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (env.type === 'message') addMessage(env.message);\n  };\n\n  // ── Render ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function addMessage(m) {\n    if (msgCount === 0) emptyEl.remove();\n    msgCount++;\n\n    const isSelf = m.from === selfID;\n    const row    = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = `msg ${isSelf ? 'self' : 'peer'}`;\n\n    const time = new Date(m.ts).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });\n    let bodyHtml;\n\n    if (m.msg_type === 'file' && m.file_id) {\n      const dlUrl = `/files/${m.file_id}/${encodeURIComponent(m.file_name)}`;\n      const icon  = fileIcon(m.file_mime || '');\n      bodyHtml =\n        `<div class=\"file-bubble\">` +\n          `<span class=\"file-icon\">${icon}</span>` +\n          `<div class=\"file-info\">` +\n            `<span class=\"file-name\" title=\"${escHtml(m.file_name)}\">${escHtml(m.file_name)}</span>` +\n            `<span class=\"file-meta\">${humanSize(m.file_size)}</span>` +\n          `</div>` +\n          `<a class=\"file-dl\" href=\"${dlUrl}\" download=\"${escHtml(m.file_name)}\">⬇ Save</a>` +\n        `</div>`;\n    } else {\n      bodyHtml = escHtml(m.body);\n    }\n\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\">${time}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">${isSelf ? 'me' : shortID(m.from)}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${bodyHtml}</span>`;\n\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addSystem(text) {\n    const row = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = 'msg system';\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\"></span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">—</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${escHtml(text)}</span>`;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addDivider(label) {\n    const d = document.createElement('div');\n    d.className = 'divider';\n    d.textContent = label;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(d);\n  }\n\n  function scrollBottom() { messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight; }\n  function shortID(id) { return id.length > 12 ? id.slice(0, 12) : id; }\n  function escHtml(s) {\n    return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')\n                    .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;');\n  }\n  function humanSize(b) {\n    if (b >= 1048576) return (b/1048576).toFixed(1) + ' MB';\n    if (b >= 1024)    return (b/1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';\n    return b + ' B';\n  }\n  function fileIcon(mime) {\n    if (mime.startsWith('image/'))  return '🖼️';\n    if (mime.startsWith('video/'))  return '🎬';\n    if (mime.startsWith('audio/'))  return '🎵';\n    if (mime.includes('pdf'))       return '📄';\n    if (mime.includes('zip') || mime.includes('compressed')) return '🗜️';\n    return '📎';\n  }\n\n  // ── Send chat ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function send() {\n    const text = inputEl.value.trim();\n    if (!text || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;\n    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'send', body: text }));\n    inputEl.value = '';\n  }\n  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', send);\n  inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') send(); });\n\n  // ── File upload ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  attachBtn.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());\n  fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {\n    if (fileInput.files.length > 0) uploadFile(fileInput.files[0]);\n    fileInput.value = '';\n  });\n\n  function uploadFile(file) {\n    if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {\n      addSystem(`❌ File too large: ${humanSize(file.size)} (max 10 MB)`);\n      return;\n    }\n    const form = new FormData();\n    form.append('file', file);\n\n    uploadLabel.textContent = `Uploading ${file.name}…`;\n    uploadProg.value = 0;\n    uploadBar.classList.add('active');\n\n    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();\n    xhr.open('POST', '/upload');\n    xhr.upload.onprogress = e => {\n      if (e.lengthComputable) uploadProg.value = (e.loaded / e.total) * 100;\n    };\n    xhr.onload = () => {\n      uploadBar.classList.remove('active');\n      if (xhr.status === 200) {\n        const res = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);\n        // Echo the file message locally (server will push to peers separately)\n        addMessage({\n          from: selfID, workspace: wsNameEl.textContent.slice(1),\n          body: `📎 ${res.file_name}`, ts: new Date().toISOString(),\n          msg_type: 'file', file_id: res.file_id,\n          file_name: res.file_name, file_mime: res.mime, file_size: res.file_size,\n        });\n      } else {\n        addSystem(`❌ Upload failed: ${xhr.responseText}`);\n      }\n    };\n    xhr.onerror = () => {\n      uploadBar.classList.remove('active');\n      addSystem('❌ Upload error');\n    };\n    xhr.send(form);\n  }\n\n  // ── Drag & drop ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  let dragCount = 0;\n  document.addEventListener('dragenter', e => {\n    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')) { dragCount++; document.body.classList.add('dragging'); }\n  });\n  document.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {\n    if (--dragCount <= 0) { dragCount = 0; document.body.classList.remove('dragging'); }\n  });\n  document.addEventListener('dragover', e => e.preventDefault());\n  document.addEventListener('drop', e => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    dragCount = 0;\n    document.body.classList.remove('dragging');\n    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];\n    if (file) uploadFile(file);\n  });\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Pushed `f87ed08`. File sharing is live:

**How it works:**
| Action | How |
|--------|-----|
| Send a file | Click 📎 or drag-and-drop onto the window |
| Max size | 10 MB per file |
| Download | Click **⬇ Save** on any received file bubble |
| File types | Auto-detected emoji icons (🖼️ image, 🎬 video, 📄 PDF, etc.) |
| Storage | Saved to `~/.groove/<workspace>/files/` on both sender and receiver |

---

**On channels/groups** — yes, this is already partially built. The `--workspace` flag is effectively a channel — each workspace name is an isolated GossipSub topic. Right now you pick it at startup. To make it dynamic (switch channels from the UI, create/join from the browser), that's **Phase 5** (workspace manager). Want me to build that next?

  "content": "package workspace\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\n\tpubsub \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n)\n\n// entry holds an active workspace topic and its local store.\ntype entry struct {\n\tws    *transport.Workspace\n\tstore *store.Store\n}\n\n// Manager owns multiple named workspace channels. It is safe for concurrent use.\ntype Manager struct {\n\tps       *pubsub.PubSub\n\tself     peer.ID\n\tstoreDir string // base dir; each channel gets storeDir/<name>\n\n\tmu     sync.RWMutex\n\tactive map[string]*entry\n}\n\n// New creates a Manager. storeDir is the base directory for per-channel stores.\nfunc New(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, storeDir string) *Manager {\n\treturn &Manager{\n\t\tps:       ps,\n\t\tself:     self,\n\t\tstoreDir: storeDir,\n\t\tactive:   make(map[string]*entry),\n\t}\n}\n\n// Join creates or returns an existing channel by name.\n// The first call opens a Badger store and subscribes to the GossipSub topic.\nfunc (m *Manager) Join(name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, error) {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tif e, ok := m.active[name]; ok {\n\t\treturn e.ws, e.store, nil\n\t}\n\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(m.storeDir, name))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open store for %q: %w\", name, err)\n\t}\n\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(m.ps, m.self, name, s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = s.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"join topic %q: %w\", name, err)\n\t}\n\n\tm.active[name] = &entry{ws: ws, store: s}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[workspace] joined channel: %s\\n\", name)\n\treturn ws, s, nil\n}\n\n// List returns the names of all currently joined channels, sorted.\nfunc (m *Manager) List() []string {\n\tm.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer m.mu.RUnlock()\n\tnames := make([]string, 0, len(m.active))\n\tfor name := range m.active {\n\t\tnames = append(names, name)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// Get returns the workspace and store for a named channel, or false if not joined.\nfunc (m *Manager) Get(name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, bool) {\n\tm.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer m.mu.RUnlock()\n\te, ok := m.active[name]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\treturn e.ws, e.store, true\n}\n\n// CloseAll shuts down all active channel stores.\nfunc (m *Manager) CloseAll() {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\tfor name, e := range m.active {\n\t\t_ = e.store.Close()\n\t\tdelete(m.active, name)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

{

(\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"embed\"\n\t\"encoding/base64\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"io\"\n\t\"io/fs\"\n\t\"mime\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\t\"github.com/google/uuid\"\n\t\"github.com/gorilla/websocket\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/workspace\"\n)\n\n//go:embed static\nvar staticFiles embed.FS\n\nconst maxUploadSize = 10 * 1024 * 1024 // 10 MB\n\n// ── WebSocket envelope types ────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ntype initEnv struct {\n\tType       string          `json:\"type\"` // \"init\"\n\tSelfID     string          `json:\"self_id\"`\n\tWorkspace  string          `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tHistory    []store.Message `json:\"history\"`\n\tWorkspaces []string        `json:\"workspaces\"`\n}\ntype msgEnv struct {\n\tType      string        `json:\"type\"` // \"message\"\n\tWorkspace string        `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tMessage   store.Message `json:\"message\"`\n}\ntype peersEnv struct {\n\tType      string `json:\"type\"` // \"peers\"\n\tWorkspace string `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tCount     int    `json:\"count\"`\n}\ntype workspacesEnv struct {\n\tType       string   `json:\"type\"` // \"workspaces\"\n\tWorkspaces []string `json:\"workspaces\"`\n}\n\n// clientMsg covers all commands the browser can send.\ntype clientMsg struct {\n\tType      string `json:\"type\"`      // \"send\" | \"join\" | \"list\"\n\tBody      string `json:\"body\"`      // for \"send\"\n\tWorkspace string `json:\"workspace\"` // for \"join\"\n}\n\nvar upgrader = websocket.Upgrader{\n\tCheckOrigin: func(r *http.Request) bool { return true },\n}\n\n// Server serves the GrooveGO web UI.\ntype Server struct {\n\tselfID   string\n\tmgr      *workspace.Manager\n\tgetPeers func(ws *transport.Workspace) int\n}\n\n// New creates a Server.\nfunc New(selfID string, mgr *workspace.Manager, getPeers func(ws *transport.Workspace) int) *Server {\n\treturn &Server{selfID: selfID, mgr: mgr, getPeers: getPeers}\n}\n\n// ListenAndServe starts the HTTP server on addr (e.g. \":8080\").\nfunc (srv *Server) ListenAndServe(ctx context.Context, addr string) error {\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\tsub, err := fs.Sub(staticFiles, \"static\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tmux.Handle(\"/\", http.FileServer(http.FS(sub)))\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/ws\", srv.handleWS)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/upload\", srv.handleUpload)\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/files/\", srv.handleFileDownload)\n\n\thttpSrv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: mux}\n\tgo func() {\n\t\t<-ctx.Done()\n\t\t_ = httpSrv.Close()\n\t}()\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[web] UI available at http://localhost%s\\n\", addr)\n\tif err := httpSrv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleWS manages a single browser connection with channel-switching support.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleWS(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tconn, err := upgrader.Upgrade(w, r, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\touterCtx, outerCancel := context.WithCancel(r.Context())\n\tdefer outerCancel()\n\n\t// Default channel from query param or \"general\"\n\tdefaultCh := r.URL.Query().Get(\"workspace\")\n\tif defaultCh == \"\" {\n\t\tdefaultCh = \"general\"\n\t}\n\n\t// Shared channel: pubsub → this connection\n\tincoming := make(chan transport.Message, 64)\n\n\t// Read browser messages in a single goroutine for the lifetime of the conn\n\tbrowserCh := make(chan clientMsg, 16)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer outerCancel()\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t_, data, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar cm clientMsg\n\t\t\tif json.Unmarshal(data, &cm) == nil {\n\t\t\t\tbrowserCh <- cm\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\t// switchTo joins a channel, sends init frame, starts receive loop.\n\t// Returns a cancel func for the previous receive goroutine.\n\tvar cancelRecv context.CancelFunc\n\tswitchTo := func(name string) {\n\t\tif cancelRecv != nil {\n\t\t\tcancelRecv()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tws, s, err := srv.mgr.Join(name)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, map[string]string{\"type\": \"error\", \"message\": err.Error()})\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\n\t\thistory, _ := s.History(name)\n\t\tif history == nil {\n\t\t\thistory = []store.Message{}\n\t\t}\n\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, initEnv{\n\t\t\tType:       \"init\",\n\t\t\tSelfID:     srv.selfID,\n\t\t\tWorkspace:  name,\n\t\t\tHistory:    history,\n\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t})\n\n\t\trecvCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(outerCtx)\n\t\tcancelRecv = cancel\n\t\tgo ws.ReadLoopInto(recvCtx, func(m transport.Message) {\n\t\t\tif m.MsgType == transport.MsgTypeFile && m.FileData != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif raw, err := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(m.FileData); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t_, _ = s.SaveFile(m.FileID, m.FileName, raw)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase incoming <- m:\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tswitchTo(defaultCh)\n\n\tpeerTick := time.NewTicker(2 * time.Second)\n\tdefer peerTick.Stop()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-outerCtx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\n\t\tcase cm := <-browserCh:\n\t\t\tswitch cm.Type {\n\t\t\tcase \"send\":\n\t\t\t\tif cm.Body == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tactiveName := defaultCh // track active channel\n\t\t\t\tif ws, s, ok := srv.mgr.Get(activeName); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tif err := ws.Publish(outerCtx, cm.Body); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, msgEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tType:      \"message\",\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tWorkspace: activeName,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\tMessage: store.Message{\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tMsgType:   transport.MsgTypeChat,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tFrom:      srv.selfID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tWorkspace: activeName,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tBody:      cm.Body,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\t\t\t_ = s // used via ws\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase \"join\":\n\t\t\t\tif cm.Workspace == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tdefaultCh = cm.Workspace\n\t\t\t\tswitchTo(cm.Workspace)\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, workspacesEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:       \"workspaces\",\n\t\t\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tcase \"list\":\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, workspacesEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:       \"workspaces\",\n\t\t\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tcase m := <-incoming:\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, msgEnv{\n\t\t\t\tType:      \"message\",\n\t\t\t\tWorkspace: m.Workspace,\n\t\t\t\tMessage:   store.Message(m),\n\t\t\t})\n\n\t\tcase <-peerTick.C:\n\t\t\tif ws, _, ok := srv.mgr.Get(defaultCh); ok {\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, peersEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:      \"peers\",\n\t\t\t\t\tWorkspace: defaultCh,\n\t\t\t\t\tCount:     srv.getPeers(ws),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\n// handleUpload accepts a multipart file POST and publishes it to the active workspace.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleUpload(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\twsName := r.URL.Query().Get(\"workspace\")\n\tif wsName == \"\" {\n\t\twsName = \"general\"\n\t}\n\tws, s, ok := srv.mgr.Get(wsName)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"not joined to workspace: \"+wsName, http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := r.ParseMultipartForm(maxUploadSize); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"file too large (max 10 MB)\", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfile, header, err := r.FormFile(\"file\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"missing file field\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(file, maxUploadSize+1))\n\tif err != nil || len(data) > maxUploadSize {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"file too large (max 10 MB)\", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tfileID := uuid.New().String()\n\tmimeType := header.Header.Get(\"Content-Type\")\n\tif mimeType == \"\" {\n\t\tmimeType = mime.TypeByExtension(filepath.Ext(header.Filename))\n\t}\n\tif mimeType == \"\" {\n\t\tmimeType = \"application/octet-stream\"\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := s.SaveFile(fileID, header.Filename, data); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"storage error\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := ws.PublishFile(r.Context(), fileID, header.Filename, mimeType, data); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"publish error: \"+err.Error(), http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{\n\t\t\"ok\": true, \"file_id\": fileID,\n\t\t\"file_name\": header.Filename, \"file_size\": len(data), \"mime\": mimeType,\n\t})\n}\n\n// handleFileDownload serves a saved file. URL: /files/{fileID}/{fileName}\nfunc (srv *Server) handleFileDownload(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\trest := r.URL.Path[len(\"/files/\"):]\n\tslash := len(rest)\n\tfor i, c := range rest {\n\t\tif c == '/' {\n\t\t\tslash = i\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif slash >= len(rest) {\n\t\thttp.NotFound(w, r)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfileID   := rest[:slash]\n\tfileName := rest[slash+1:]\n\n\t// Search all joined workspaces for the file\n\tfor _, name := range srv.mgr.List() {\n\t\tif _, s, ok := srv.mgr.Get(name); ok {\n\t\t\tpath := s.FilePath(fileID, fileName)\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\",\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename=\"%s\"`, filepath.Base(fileName)))\n\t\t\thttp.ServeFile(w, r, path)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\thttp.NotFound(w, r)\n}\n\nfunc (srv *Server) writeJSON(conn *websocket.Conn, v any) {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(v)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t_ = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, data)\n}\n"
}

{

  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/web\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/workspace\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"libp2p TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\thttpAddr  := flag.String(\"http\", \":8080\", \"Web UI listen address\")\n\tdefaultCh := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Default channel to join on startup\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Base directory for local stores\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// GossipSub router\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Workspace manager — handles all channels\n\tmgr := workspace.New(ps, n.ID, *dataDir)\n\tdefer mgr.CloseAll()\n\n\t// Join default channel on startup\n\tif _, _, err := mgr.Join(*defaultCh); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tgetPeers := func(ws *transport.Workspace) int { return len(ws.ListPeers()) }\n\n\twebSrv := web.New(n.ID.String(), mgr, getPeers)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tif err := webSrv.ListenAndServe(ctx, *httpAddr); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[web] %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove-web] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}

#5848cc;\n      --text:        #1e2130;\n      --muted:       #7c85a2;\n      --me:          #6d5ce7;\n      --peer:        #0284c7;\n      --system:      #94a3b8;\n      --success:     #16a34a;\n      --danger:      #dc2626;\n      --file-bg:     #f0fdf0;\n      --file-border: #bbf7d0;\n      --ch-active:   #dde1ef;\n      --ch-hover:    #e8eaf4;\n      --shadow:      0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.1);\n    }\n\n    body {\n      background: var(--bg);\n      color: var(--text);\n      font-family: 'Inter', 'Segoe UI', system-ui, sans-serif;\n      height: 100dvh;\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      transition: background .25s, color .25s;\n    }\n\n    /* ── App shell: sidebar + main ── */\n    .app {\n      display: flex;\n      flex: 1;\n      overflow: hidden;\n    }\n\n    /* ── Sidebar ── */\n    .sidebar {\n      width: 200px;\n      min-width: 200px;\n      background: var(--sidebar-bg);\n      border-right: 1px solid var(--border);\n      display: flex;\n      flex-direction: column;\n      overflow: hidden;\n    }\n    .sidebar-header {\n      padding: 14px 14px 8px;\n      font-size: .7rem;\n      font-weight: 700;\n      text-transform: uppercase;\n      letter-spacing: .08em;\n      color: var(--muted);\n      display: flex;\n      align-items: center;\n      justify-content: space-between;\n    }\n    .sidebar-add {\n      background: none; border: none; cursor: pointer;\n      color: var(--muted); font-size: 1.1rem; line-height: 1;\n      padding: 2px 4px; border-radius: 4px;\n      transition: color .15s, background .15s;\n    }\n    .sidebar-add:hover { color: var(--accent); background: var(--ch-hover); }\n\n    .channel-list {\n      flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; padding: 0 6px 8px;\n    }\n    .channel-list::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; }\n    .channel-list::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 2px; }\n\n    .channel-item {\n      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px;\n      padding: 7px 10px; border-radius: 8px;\n      cursor: pointer; font-size: .88rem; color: var(--muted);\n      transition: background .12s, color .12s;\n      user-select: none;\n    }\n    .channel-item:hover { background: var(--ch-hover); color: var(--text); }\n    .channel-item.active {\n      background: var(--ch-active); color: var(--text); font-weight: 600;\n    }\n    .channel-item.active .ch-hash { color: var(--accent); }\n    .ch-hash { font-weight: 700; color: var(--muted); font-size: .9rem; }\n    .ch-unread {\n      margin-left: auto; background: var(--accent);\n      color: #fff; font-size: .65rem; font-weight: 700;\n      border-radius: 10px; padding: 1px 6px; min-width: 18px;\n      text-align: center; display: none;\n    }\n    .ch-unread.show { display: block; }\n\n    /* New channel form */\n    .new-channel-form {\n      display: none; padding: 8px 10px; gap: 6px;\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n    }\n    .new-channel-form.open { display: flex; }\n    .new-channel-input {\n      flex: 1; background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 8px; color: var(--text); font-size: .85rem;\n      padding: 6px 10px; outline: none;\n      transition: border-color .2s;\n    }\n    .new-channel-input:focus { border-color: var(--accent); }\n    .new-channel-btn {\n      background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none;\n      border-radius: 8px; padding: 6px 12px; font-size: .8rem;\n      font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; transition: opacity .2s;\n    }\n    .new-channel-btn:hover { opacity: .85; }\n\n    /* ── Main area ── */\n    .main {\n      flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column; overflow: hidden;\n    }\n\n    /* ── Header ── */\n    header {\n      display: flex; align-items: center;\n      padding: 0 20px; height: 56px;\n      background: var(--surface);\n      border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border);\n      box-shadow: var(--shadow); flex-shrink: 0; gap: 12px;\n    }\n    .logo { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }\n    .logo-icon {\n      width: 30px; height: 30px; background: var(--accent);\n      border-radius: 8px; display: flex; align-items: center;\n      justify-content: center; font-size: 16px; flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    .logo-text { font-size: 1rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--text); }\n    .logo-text span { color: var(--accent); }\n    #ws-name {\n      font-size: .85rem; font-weight: 700; color: var(--accent);\n    }\n    .header-right { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; margin-left: auto; }\n    #peers-badge {\n      font-size: .75rem; font-weight: 500; color: var(--muted);\n      background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 20px; padding: 3px 12px; transition: color .2s;\n    }\n    #peers-badge.has-peers { color: var(--success); border-color: var(--success); }\n    #status-dot {\n      width: 8px; height: 8px; border-radius: 50%;\n      background: var(--danger); transition: background .3s; flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    #status-dot.connected { background: var(--success); }\n    #theme-btn {\n      background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 8px; color: var(--muted); cursor: pointer;\n      font-size: 1rem; width: 32px; height: 32px;\n      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      transition: color .2s, border-color .2s;\n    }\n    #theme-btn:hover { color: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); }\n\n    /* ── Drop overlay ── */\n    #drop-overlay {\n      display: none; position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 100;\n      background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, transparent);\n      border: 3px dashed var(--accent);\n      align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      font-size: 1.4rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--accent);\n      pointer-events: none;\n    }\n    body.dragging #drop-overlay { display: flex; }\n\n    /* ── Messages ── */\n    #messages {\n      flex: 1; overflow-y: auto; padding: 20px;\n      display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px;\n    }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 5px; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-track { background: transparent; }\n    #messages::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: var(--border); border-radius: 3px; }\n\n    .msg {\n      display: grid; grid-template-columns: 42px 90px 1fr;\n      align-items: baseline; gap: 0 8px;\n      padding: 4px 8px; border-radius: 8px; transition: background .12s;\n    }\n    .msg:hover { background: var(--surface2); }\n    .msg.self   .sender { color: var(--me); }\n    .msg.peer   .sender { color: var(--peer); }\n    .msg.system .sender { color: var(--system); font-style: italic; }\n    .timestamp { font-size: .7rem; color: var(--muted); text-align: right; white-space: nowrap; }\n    .sender { font-weight: 600; font-size: .82rem; white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }\n    .body { font-size: .9rem; word-break: break-word; line-height: 1.5; }\n\n    /* ── File bubble ── */\n    .file-bubble {\n      display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      background: var(--file-bg); border: 1px solid var(--file-border);\n      border-radius: 10px; padding: 8px 14px; margin-top: 2px; max-width: 320px;\n    }\n    .file-icon { font-size: 1.4rem; flex-shrink: 0; }\n    .file-info { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-width: 0; }\n    .file-name { font-size: .85rem; font-weight: 600; color: var(--text); white-space: nowrap; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; }\n    .file-meta { font-size: .72rem; color: var(--muted); }\n    .file-dl {\n      margin-left: auto; flex-shrink: 0;\n      background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none;\n      border-radius: 8px; padding: 5px 12px; font-size: .8rem;\n      font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; text-decoration: none;\n      transition: opacity .2s;\n    }\n    .file-dl:hover { opacity: .85; }\n\n    .divider {\n      display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      color: var(--muted); font-size: .7rem;\n      text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .06em; margin: 10px 8px;\n    }\n    .divider::before, .divider::after { content: ''; flex: 1; height: 1px; background: var(--border); }\n\n    #empty {\n      flex: 1; display: flex; flex-direction: column;\n      align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      color: var(--muted); gap: 8px; font-size: .9rem; user-select: none;\n    }\n    #empty .icon { font-size: 2.5rem; opacity: .4; }\n\n    /* ── Upload progress ── */\n    #upload-bar {\n      display: none; align-items: center; gap: 10px;\n      padding: 6px 20px; background: var(--surface2);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border); font-size: .8rem; color: var(--muted);\n    }\n    #upload-bar.active { display: flex; }\n    #upload-progress { flex: 1; height: 4px; border-radius: 2px; accent-color: var(--accent); }\n\n    /* ── Input bar ── */\n    footer {\n      padding: 12px 20px; background: var(--surface);\n      border-top: 1px solid var(--border);\n      display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-shrink: 0; align-items: center;\n    }\n    #attach-btn {\n      background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 10px; color: var(--muted); cursor: pointer;\n      font-size: 1.1rem; width: 42px; height: 42px;\n      display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;\n      transition: color .2s, border-color .2s; flex-shrink: 0;\n    }\n    #attach-btn:hover { color: var(--accent); border-color: var(--accent); }\n    #file-input { display: none; }\n    #input {\n      flex: 1; background: var(--surface2); border: 1px solid var(--border);\n      border-radius: 10px; color: var(--text); font-size: .92rem;\n      padding: 10px 16px; outline: none;\n      transition: border-color .2s, box-shadow .2s;\n    }\n    #input:focus {\n      border-color: var(--accent);\n      box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px color-mix(in srgb, var(--accent) 15%, transparent);\n    }\n    #input::placeholder { color: var(--muted); }\n    #send {\n      background: var(--accent); color: #fff; border: none;\n      border-radius: 10px; padding: 10px 22px; font-size: .9rem;\n      font-weight: 600; cursor: pointer; white-space: nowrap;\n      transition: background .2s, transform .1s;\n    }\n    #send:hover:not(:disabled) { background: var(--accent-h); }\n    #send:active:not(:disabled) { transform: scale(.97); }\n    #send:disabled { opacity: .35; cursor: default; }\n\n    /* ── Responsive: hide sidebar on small screens ── */\n    @media (max-width: 540px) {\n      .sidebar { display: none; }\n    }\n  </style>\n</head>\n<body>\n\n<div id=\"drop-overlay\">📎 Drop file to send</div>\n\n<div class=\"app\">\n  <!-- ── Sidebar ── -->\n  <aside class=\"sidebar\">\n    <div class=\"sidebar-header\">\n      Channels\n      <button class=\"sidebar-add\" id=\"add-ch-btn\" title=\"New channel\">＋</button>\n    </div>\n    <div class=\"channel-list\" id=\"channel-list\"></div>\n    <div class=\"new-channel-form\" id=\"new-ch-form\">\n      <input class=\"new-channel-input\" id=\"new-ch-input\" placeholder=\"channel-name\" maxlength=\"32\" />\n      <button class=\"new-channel-btn\" id=\"new-ch-submit\">Join</button>\n    </div>\n  </aside>\n\n  <!-- ── Main ── -->\n  <div class=\"main\">\n    <header>\n      <div class=\"logo\">\n        <div class=\"logo-icon\">🎵</div>\n        <span class=\"logo-text\">Groove<span>GO</span></span>\n      </div>\n      <span id=\"ws-name\">#general</span>\n      <div class=\"header-right\">\n        <div id=\"peers-badge\">0 peers</div>\n        <div id=\"status-dot\"></div>\n        <button id=\"theme-btn\" title=\"Toggle light/dark\">🌙</button>\n      </div>\n    </header>\n\n    <div id=\"upload-bar\">\n      <span id=\"upload-label\">Uploading…</span>\n      <progress id=\"upload-progress\" max=\"100\" value=\"0\"></progress>\n    </div>\n\n    <div id=\"messages\">\n      <div id=\"empty\">\n        <div class=\"icon\">💬</div>\n        <div>No messages yet — say hello or drop a file!</div>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <footer>\n      <button id=\"attach-btn\" title=\"Send a file\">📎</button>\n      <input id=\"file-input\" type=\"file\" />\n      <input id=\"input\" type=\"text\" placeholder=\"Type a message…\" autocomplete=\"off\" />\n      <button id=\"send\" disabled>Send</button>\n    </footer>\n  </div>\n</div>\n\n<script>\n  const messagesEl   = document.getElementById('messages');\n  const inputEl      = document.getElementById('input');\n  const sendBtn      = document.getElementById('send');\n  const dot          = document.getElementById('status-dot');\n  const peersBadge   = document.getElementById('peers-badge');\n  const wsNameEl     = document.getElementById('ws-name');\n  const themeBtn     = document.getElementById('theme-btn');\n  const html         = document.documentElement;\n  const attachBtn    = document.getElementById('attach-btn');\n  const fileInput    = document.getElementById('file-input');\n  const uploadBar    = document.getElementById('upload-bar');\n  const uploadLabel  = document.getElementById('upload-label');\n  const uploadProg   = document.getElementById('upload-progress');\n  const channelList  = document.getElementById('channel-list');\n  const addChBtn     = document.getElementById('add-ch-btn');\n  const newChForm    = document.getElementById('new-ch-form');\n  const newChInput   = document.getElementById('new-ch-input');\n  const newChSubmit  = document.getElementById('new-ch-submit');\n\n  let selfID       = '';\n  let activeChannel = 'general';\n  // per-channel message counts (for empty state)\n  const channelMsgs = {};\n\n  // ── Theme ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  setTheme(localStorage.getItem('groove-theme') || 'dark');\n  themeBtn.addEventListener('click', () =>\n    setTheme(html.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'));\n  function setTheme(t) {\n    html.dataset.theme = t;\n    themeBtn.textContent = t === 'dark' ? '☀️' : '🌙';\n    localStorage.setItem('groove-theme', t);\n  }\n\n  // ── WebSocket ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  const proto = location.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss' : 'ws';\n  const wsUrl = `${proto}://${location.host}/ws?workspace=${encodeURIComponent(activeChannel)}`;\n  const ws    = new WebSocket(wsUrl);\n\n  ws.onopen = () => {\n    dot.classList.add('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = false;\n    addSystem('Connected to node');\n  };\n  ws.onclose = () => {\n    dot.classList.remove('connected');\n    sendBtn.disabled = true;\n    addSystem('Disconnected — refresh to reconnect');\n  };\n  ws.onerror = () => addSystem('WebSocket error');\n\n  ws.onmessage = (evt) => {\n    const env = JSON.parse(evt.data);\n\n    if (env.type === 'init') {\n      selfID = env.self_id;\n      activeChannel = env.workspace;\n      wsNameEl.textContent = '#' + env.workspace;\n      clearMessages();\n      if (env.history && env.history.length > 0) {\n        addDivider(`${env.history.length} previous message${env.history.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`);\n        env.history.forEach(m => addMessage(m, env.workspace));\n        addDivider('live');\n      }\n      if (env.workspaces) updateSidebar(env.workspaces, env.workspace);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (env.type === 'workspaces') {\n      updateSidebar(env.workspaces, activeChannel);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (env.type === 'peers') {\n      if (env.workspace === activeChannel) {\n        const n = env.count;\n        peersBadge.textContent = `${n} peer${n !== 1 ? 's' : ''}`;\n        peersBadge.classList.toggle('has-peers', n > 0);\n      }\n      return;\n    }\n    if (env.type === 'message') {\n      if (env.workspace === activeChannel) {\n        addMessage(env.message, env.workspace);\n      } else {\n        // Show unread badge on other channel\n        bumpUnread(env.workspace);\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  // ── Sidebar ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function updateSidebar(channels, active) {\n    // preserve unread counts\n    const existing = {};\n    channelList.querySelectorAll('.channel-item').forEach(el => {\n      const badge = el.querySelector('.ch-unread');\n      existing[el.dataset.ch] = badge ? parseInt(badge.textContent) || 0 : 0;\n    });\n    channelList.innerHTML = '';\n    const sorted = [...new Set(channels)].sort();\n    sorted.forEach(name => {\n      const item = document.createElement('div');\n      item.className = 'channel-item' + (name === active ? ' active' : '');\n      item.dataset.ch = name;\n      const unread = existing[name] || 0;\n      item.innerHTML =\n        `<span class=\"ch-hash\">#</span>${escHtml(name)}` +\n        `<span class=\"ch-unread${unread > 0 ? ' show' : ''}\">${unread}</span>`;\n      item.addEventListener('click', () => joinChannel(name));\n      channelList.appendChild(item);\n    });\n  }\n\n  function bumpUnread(channelName) {\n    const item = channelList.querySelector(`[data-ch=\"${CSS.escape(channelName)}\"]`);\n    if (!item) return;\n    const badge = item.querySelector('.ch-unread');\n    const cur = parseInt(badge.textContent) || 0;\n    badge.textContent = cur + 1;\n    badge.classList.add('show');\n  }\n\n  function joinChannel(name) {\n    if (name === activeChannel) return;\n    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'join', workspace: name }));\n    // Optimistically mark active in sidebar\n    channelList.querySelectorAll('.channel-item').forEach(el => {\n      el.classList.toggle('active', el.dataset.ch === name);\n    });\n    // Clear unread for this channel\n    const item = channelList.querySelector(`[data-ch=\"${CSS.escape(name)}\"]`);\n    if (item) {\n      const badge = item.querySelector('.ch-unread');\n      badge.textContent = '0';\n      badge.classList.remove('show');\n    }\n  }\n\n  // New channel form\n  addChBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {\n    newChForm.classList.toggle('open');\n    if (newChForm.classList.contains('open')) newChInput.focus();\n  });\n  newChSubmit.addEventListener('click', createChannel);\n  newChInput.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') createChannel(); });\n  function createChannel() {\n    const name = newChInput.value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-_]/g, '-');\n    if (!name) return;\n    newChInput.value = '';\n    newChForm.classList.remove('open');\n    joinChannel(name);\n  }\n\n  // ── Messages ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function clearMessages() {\n    messagesEl.innerHTML = '<div id=\"empty\"><div class=\"icon\">💬</div><div>No messages yet — say hello or drop a file!</div></div>';\n    channelMsgs[activeChannel] = 0;\n  }\n\n  function addMessage(m, forChannel) {\n    const emptyEl = document.getElementById('empty');\n    if (emptyEl) emptyEl.remove();\n\n    const isSelf = m.from === selfID;\n    const row    = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = `msg ${isSelf ? 'self' : 'peer'}`;\n\n    const time = new Date(m.ts).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });\n    let bodyHtml;\n\n    if (m.msg_type === 'file' && m.file_id) {\n      const dlUrl = `/files/${m.file_id}/${encodeURIComponent(m.file_name)}`;\n      bodyHtml =\n        `<div class=\"file-bubble\">` +\n          `<span class=\"file-icon\">${fileIcon(m.file_mime || '')}</span>` +\n          `<div class=\"file-info\">` +\n            `<span class=\"file-name\" title=\"${escHtml(m.file_name)}\">${escHtml(m.file_name)}</span>` +\n            `<span class=\"file-meta\">${humanSize(m.file_size)}</span>` +\n          `</div>` +\n          `<a class=\"file-dl\" href=\"${dlUrl}\" download=\"${escHtml(m.file_name)}\">⬇ Save</a>` +\n        `</div>`;\n    } else {\n      bodyHtml = escHtml(m.body);\n    }\n\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\">${time}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">${isSelf ? 'me' : shortID(m.from)}</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${bodyHtml}</span>`;\n\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addSystem(text) {\n    const row = document.createElement('div');\n    row.className = 'msg system';\n    row.innerHTML =\n      `<span class=\"timestamp\"></span>` +\n      `<span class=\"sender\">—</span>` +\n      `<span class=\"body\">${escHtml(text)}</span>`;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(row);\n    scrollBottom();\n  }\n\n  function addDivider(label) {\n    const d = document.createElement('div');\n    d.className = 'divider';\n    d.textContent = label;\n    messagesEl.appendChild(d);\n  }\n\n  function scrollBottom() { messagesEl.scrollTop = messagesEl.scrollHeight; }\n\n  // ── Send ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function send() {\n    const text = inputEl.value.trim();\n    if (!text || ws.readyState !== WebSocket.OPEN) return;\n    ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'send', body: text }));\n    inputEl.value = '';\n  }\n  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', send);\n  inputEl.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Enter') send(); });\n\n  // ── File upload ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  attachBtn.addEventListener('click', () => fileInput.click());\n  fileInput.addEventListener('change', () => {\n    if (fileInput.files.length > 0) uploadFile(fileInput.files[0]);\n    fileInput.value = '';\n  });\n\n  function uploadFile(file) {\n    if (file.size > 10 * 1024 * 1024) {\n      addSystem(`❌ File too large: ${humanSize(file.size)} (max 10 MB)`);\n      return;\n    }\n    const form = new FormData();\n    form.append('file', file);\n\n    uploadLabel.textContent = `Uploading ${file.name}…`;\n    uploadProg.value = 0;\n    uploadBar.classList.add('active');\n\n    const xhr = new XMLHttpRequest();\n    xhr.open('POST', `/upload?workspace=${encodeURIComponent(activeChannel)}`);\n    xhr.upload.onprogress = e => {\n      if (e.lengthComputable) uploadProg.value = (e.loaded / e.total) * 100;\n    };\n    xhr.onload = () => {\n      uploadBar.classList.remove('active');\n      if (xhr.status === 200) {\n        const res = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText);\n        addMessage({\n          from: selfID, workspace: activeChannel,\n          body: `📎 ${res.file_name}`, ts: new Date().toISOString(),\n          msg_type: 'file', file_id: res.file_id,\n          file_name: res.file_name, file_mime: res.mime, file_size: res.file_size,\n        }, activeChannel);\n      } else {\n        addSystem(`❌ Upload failed: ${xhr.responseText}`);\n      }\n    };\n    xhr.onerror = () => {\n      uploadBar.classList.remove('active');\n      addSystem('❌ Upload error');\n    };\n    xhr.send(form);\n  }\n\n  // ── Drag & drop ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  let dragCount = 0;\n  document.addEventListener('dragenter', e => {\n    if (e.dataTransfer.types.includes('Files')) { dragCount++; document.body.classList.add('dragging'); }\n  });\n  document.addEventListener('dragleave', () => {\n    if (--dragCount <= 0) { dragCount = 0; document.body.classList.remove('dragging'); }\n  });\n  document.addEventListener('dragover', e => e.preventDefault());\n  document.addEventListener('drop', e => {\n    e.preventDefault(); dragCount = 0; document.body.classList.remove('dragging');\n    const file = e.dataTransfer.files[0];\n    if (file) uploadFile(file);\n  });\n\n  // ── Helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n  function shortID(id) { return id.length > 12 ? id.slice(0, 12) : id; }\n  function escHtml(s) {\n    return String(s).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;')\n                    .replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/\"/g,'&quot;');\n  }\n  function humanSize(b) {\n    if (b >= 1048576) return (b/1048576).toFixed(1) + ' MB';\n    if (b >= 1024)    return (b/1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';\n    return b + ' B';\n  }\n  function fileIcon(mime) {\n    if (mime.startsWith('image/'))  return '🖼️';\n    if (mime.startsWith('video/'))  return '🎬';\n    if (mime.startsWith('audio/'))  return '🎵';\n    if (mime.includes('pdf'))       return '📄';\n    if (mime.includes('zip') || mime.includes('compressed')) return '🗜️';\n    return '📎';\n  }\n</script>\n</body>\n</html>\n"
}

**What's new:**

```
┌──────────┬────────────────────────────────────┐

│ #safecast│  12:04  QmXf3r:  hi there!         │

│ ──────── │  [📎]  [Type a message…]   [Send]  │
│   ＋      │                                    │
└──────────┴────────────────────────────────────┘
```

| Feature | Detail |
|---------|--------|
| Channel sidebar | Lists all joined channels, highlights active one |
| Switch channels | Click any channel — messages load instantly |
| Create channel | Click ＋, type a name, press Enter |
| Unread badges | Red count on channels with new messages while you're elsewhere |
| Per-channel store | Each channel has its own isolated Badger DB |
| Responsive | Sidebar hidden on mobile screens |

**Remaining phases:** Phase 6 (presence/online status) and Phase 7 (NAT traversal for internet-wide peers). Want to continue?
