{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/presence/tracker.go",
  "content": "package presence\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"encoding/json\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\t\"time\"\n\n\tpubsub \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n)\n\nconst (\n\theartbeatInterval = 5 * time.Second\n\tofflineThreshold  = 15 * time.Second // missed 3 heartbeats = offline\n)\n\n// beat is the heartbeat wire format.\ntype beat struct {\n\tPeerID    string    `json:\"peer_id\"`\n\tWorkspace string    `json:\"workspace\"`\n\tTimestamp time.Time `json:\"ts\"`\n}\n\n// PeerInfo holds the last-seen time for a peer.\ntype PeerInfo struct {\n\tID       peer.ID\n\tLastSeen time.Time\n}\n\n// Tracker publishes heartbeats and tracks which peers are online.\ntype Tracker struct {\n\tself      peer.ID\n\tworkspace string\n\ttopic     *pubsub.Topic\n\tsub       *pubsub.Subscription\n\n\tmu    sync.RWMutex\n\tpeers map[peer.ID]time.Time\n}\n\n// New creates and starts a Tracker for the given workspace pubsub instance.\n// It joins a dedicated presence topic (separate from the chat topic).\nfunc New(ctx context.Context, ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, workspace string) (*Tracker, error) {\n\ttopicName := \"presence-\" + workspace\n\ttopic, err := ps.Join(topicName)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"presence join topic: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\tsub, err := topic.Subscribe()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"presence subscribe: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tt := &Tracker{\n\t\tself:      self,\n\t\tworkspace: workspace,\n\t\ttopic:     topic,\n\t\tsub:       sub,\n\t\tpeers:     make(map[peer.ID]time.Time),\n\t}\n\n\tgo t.publishLoop(ctx)\n\tgo t.receiveLoop(ctx)\n\tgo t.reapLoop(ctx)\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[presence] tracking started for workspace: %s\\n\", workspace)\n\treturn t, nil\n}\n\n// Online returns peer IDs seen within the offline threshold, excluding self.\nfunc (t *Tracker) Online() []PeerInfo {\n\tt.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer t.mu.RUnlock()\n\tcutoff := time.Now().Add(-offlineThreshold)\n\tout := make([]PeerInfo, 0, len(t.peers))\n\tfor id, lastSeen := range t.peers {\n\t\tif lastSeen.After(cutoff) {\n\t\t\tout = append(out, PeerInfo{ID: id, LastSeen: lastSeen})\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn out\n}\n\n// publishLoop sends a heartbeat every heartbeatInterval.\nfunc (t *Tracker) publishLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(heartbeatInterval)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\tt.sendBeat(ctx) // send immediately on start\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tt.sendBeat(ctx)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (t *Tracker) sendBeat(ctx context.Context) {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(beat{\n\t\tPeerID:    t.self.String(),\n\t\tWorkspace: t.workspace,\n\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t_ = t.topic.Publish(ctx, data)\n}\n\n// receiveLoop listens for heartbeats from other peers and updates the map.\nfunc (t *Tracker) receiveLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tfor {\n\t\tm, err := t.sub.Next(ctx)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tvar b beat\n\t\tif err := json.Unmarshal(m.Data, &b); err != nil {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tid, err := peer.Decode(b.PeerID)\n\t\tif err != nil || id == t.self {\n\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t}\n\t\tt.mu.Lock()\n\t\tt.peers[id] = time.Now()\n\t\tt.mu.Unlock()\n\t}\n}\n\n// reapLoop removes peers that haven't been seen in a while.\nfunc (t *Tracker) reapLoop(ctx context.Context) {\n\tticker := time.NewTicker(offlineThreshold)\n\tdefer ticker.Stop()\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-ctx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\tcase <-ticker.C:\n\t\t\tcutoff := time.Now().Add(-offlineThreshold)\n\t\t\tt.mu.Lock()\n\t\t\tfor id, lastSeen := range t.peers {\n\t\t\t\tif lastSeen.Before(cutoff) {\n\t\t\t\t\tdelete(t.peers, id)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tt.mu.Unlock()\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n"
}

```
1	package workspace
2	
3	import (
4		"fmt"
5		"path/filepath"
6		"sync"
7	
8		pubsub "github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub"
9		"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer"
10		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store"
11		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport"
12	)
13	
14	// entry holds an active workspace topic and its local store.
15	type entry struct {
16		ws    *transport.Workspace
17		store *store.Store
18	}
19	
20	// Manager owns multiple named workspace channels. It is safe for concurrent use.
21	type Manager struct {
22		ps       *pubsub.PubSub
23		self     peer.ID
24		storeDir string // base dir; each channel gets storeDir/<name>
25	
26		mu     sync.RWMutex
27		active map[string]*entry
28	}
29	
30	// New creates a Manager. storeDir is the base directory for per-channel stores.
31	func New(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, storeDir string) *Manager {
32		return &Manager{
33			ps:       ps,
34			self:     self,
35			storeDir: storeDir,
36			active:   make(map[string]*entry),
37		}
38	}
39	
40	// Join creates or returns an existing channel by name.
41	// The first call opens a Badger store and subscribes to the GossipSub topic.
42	func (m *Manager) Join(name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, error) {
43		m.mu.Lock()
44		defer m.mu.Unlock()
45	
46		if e, ok := m.active[name]; ok {
47			return e.ws, e.store, nil
48		}
49	
50		s, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(m.storeDir, name))
51		if err != nil {
52			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("open store for %q: %w", name, err)
53		}
54	
55		ws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(m.ps, m.self, name, s)
56		if err != nil {
57			_ = s.Close()
58			return nil, nil, fmt.Errorf("join topic %q: %w", name, err)
59		}
60	
61		m.active[name] = &entry{ws: ws, store: s}
62		fmt.Printf("[workspace] joined channel: %s\n", name)
63		return ws, s, nil
64	}
65	
66	// List returns the names of all currently joined channels, sorted.
67	func (m *Manager) List() []string {
68		m.mu.RLock()
69		defer m.mu.RUnlock()
70		names := make([]string, 0, len(m.active))
71		for name := range m.active {
72			names = append(names, name)
73		}
74		return names
75	}
76	
77	// Get returns the workspace and store for a named channel, or false if not joined.
78	func (m *Manager) Get(name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, bool) {
79		m.mu.RLock()
80		defer m.mu.RUnlock()
81		e, ok := m.active[name]
82		if !ok {
83			return nil, nil, false
84		}
85		return e.ws, e.store, true
86	}
87	
88	// CloseAll shuts down all active channel stores.
89	func (m *Manager) CloseAll() {
90		m.mu.Lock()
91		defer m.mu.Unlock()
92		for name, e := range m.active {
93			_ = e.store.Close()
94			delete(m.active, name)
95		}
96	}
97

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/workspace/manager.go",
  "content": "package workspace\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\n\tpubsub \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/presence\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n)\n\n// entry holds an active workspace topic, its local store, and presence tracker.\ntype entry struct {\n\tws      *transport.Workspace\n\tstore   *store.Store\n\ttracker *presence.Tracker\n}\n\n// Manager owns multiple named workspace channels. It is safe for concurrent use.\ntype Manager struct {\n\tps       *pubsub.PubSub\n\tself     peer.ID\n\tstoreDir string\n\n\tmu     sync.RWMutex\n\tactive map[string]*entry\n}\n\n// New creates a Manager. storeDir is the base directory for per-channel stores.\nfunc New(ps *pubsub.PubSub, self peer.ID, storeDir string) *Manager {\n\treturn &Manager{\n\t\tps:       ps,\n\t\tself:     self,\n\t\tstoreDir: storeDir,\n\t\tactive:   make(map[string]*entry),\n\t}\n}\n\n// Join creates or returns an existing channel by name.\nfunc (m *Manager) Join(ctx context.Context, name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, *presence.Tracker, error) {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\n\tif e, ok := m.active[name]; ok {\n\t\treturn e.ws, e.store, e.tracker, nil\n\t}\n\n\ts, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(m.storeDir, name))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"open store for %q: %w\", name, err)\n\t}\n\n\tws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(m.ps, m.self, name, s)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = s.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"join topic %q: %w\", name, err)\n\t}\n\n\ttracker, err := presence.New(ctx, m.ps, m.self, name)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\t_ = s.Close()\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, fmt.Errorf(\"presence for %q: %w\", name, err)\n\t}\n\n\tm.active[name] = &entry{ws: ws, store: s, tracker: tracker}\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[workspace] joined channel: %s\\n\", name)\n\treturn ws, s, tracker, nil\n}\n\n// List returns the names of all currently joined channels.\nfunc (m *Manager) List() []string {\n\tm.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer m.mu.RUnlock()\n\tnames := make([]string, 0, len(m.active))\n\tfor name := range m.active {\n\t\tnames = append(names, name)\n\t}\n\treturn names\n}\n\n// Get returns the workspace, store and tracker for a channel, or false if not joined.\nfunc (m *Manager) Get(name string) (*transport.Workspace, *store.Store, *presence.Tracker, bool) {\n\tm.mu.RLock()\n\tdefer m.mu.RUnlock()\n\te, ok := m.active[name]\n\tif !ok {\n\t\treturn nil, nil, nil, false\n\t}\n\treturn e.ws, e.store, e.tracker, true\n}\n\n// CloseAll shuts down all active channel stores.\nfunc (m *Manager) CloseAll() {\n\tm.mu.Lock()\n\tdefer m.mu.Unlock()\n\tfor name, e := range m.active {\n\t\t_ = e.store.Close()\n\t\tdelete(m.active, name)\n\t}\n}\n"
}

http.NewServeMux()\n\tsub, err := fs.Sub(staticFiles, \"static\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\tmux.Handle(\"/\", http.FileServer(http.FS(sub)))\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"/ws\", {\n\t\t<-ctx.Done()\n\t\t_ = httpSrv.Close()\n\t}()\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[web] UI available at http://localhost%s\\n\", addr)\n\tif err := httpSrv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {\n\t\treturn err\n\t}\n\treturn nil\n}\n\n// handleWS manages a single browser connection with channel-switching and presence.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleWS(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tconn, err := upgrader.Upgrade(w, r, nil)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer conn.Close()\n\n\touterCtx, outerCancel := context.WithCancel(r.Context())\n\tdefer outerCancel()\n\n\tdefaultCh := r.URL.Query().Get(\"workspace\")\n\tif defaultCh == \"\" {\n\t\tdefaultCh = \"general\"\n\t}\n\n\tincoming := make(chan transport.Message, 64)\n\n\t// Browser reader goroutine\n\tbrowserCh := make(chan clientMsg, 16)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tdefer outerCancel()\n\t\tfor {\n\t\t\t_, data, err := conn.ReadMessage()\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tvar cm clientMsg\n\t\t\tif json.Unmarshal(data, &cm) == nil {\n\t\t\t\tbrowserCh <- cm\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\t// switchTo joins a channel, sends init + presence, starts receive loop.\n\tvar cancelRecv context.CancelFunc\n\tvar activeTracker *presence.Tracker\n\n\tswitchTo := func(name string) {\n\t\tif cancelRecv != nil {\n\t\t\tcancelRecv()\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tws, s, tracker, err := srv.mgr.Join(outerCtx, name)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, map[string]string{\"type\": \"error\", \"message\": err.Error()})\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t\tactiveTracker = tracker\n\n\t\thistory, _ := s.History(name)\n\t\tif history == nil {\n\t\t\thistory = []store.Message{}\n\t\t}\n\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, initEnv{\n\t\t\tType:       \"init\",\n\t\t\tSelfID:     srv.selfID,\n\t\t\tWorkspace:  name,\n\t\t\tHistory:    history,\n\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t})\n\t\t// Send initial presence\n\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, srv.presenceEnv(name, tracker))\n\n\t\trecvCtx, cancel := context.WithCancel(outerCtx)\n\t\tcancelRecv = cancel\n\t\tgo ws.ReadLoopInto(recvCtx, func(m transport.Message) {\n\t\t\tif m.MsgType == transport.MsgTypeFile && m.FileData != \"\" {\n\t\t\t\tif raw, decErr := base64.StdEncoding.DecodeString(m.FileData); decErr == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t_, _ = s.SaveFile(m.FileID, m.FileName, raw)\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tselect {\n\t\t\tcase incoming <- m:\n\t\t\tdefault:\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t})\n\t}\n\n\tswitchTo(defaultCh)\n\n\ttick := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Second)\n\tdefer tick.Stop()\n\n\tfor {\n\t\tselect {\n\t\tcase <-outerCtx.Done():\n\t\t\treturn\n\n\t\tcase cm := <-browserCh:\n\t\t\tswitch cm.Type {\n\t\t\tcase \"send\":\n\t\t\t\tif cm.Body == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tif ws, s, _, ok := srv.mgr.Get(defaultCh); ok {\n\t\t\t\t\tif err := ws.Publish(outerCtx, cm.Body); err == nil {\n\t\t\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn,  srv.selfID,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tWorkspace: defaultCh,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tBody:      cm.Body,\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\tTimestamp: time.Now().UTC(),\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t},\n\t\t\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t\t\t\t_ = s\n\t\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tcase \"join\":\n\t\t\t\tif cm.Workspace == \"\" {\n\t\t\t\t\tcontinue\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tdefaultCh = cm.Workspace\n\t\t\t\tswitchTo(cm.Workspace)\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, workspacesEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:       \"workspaces\",\n\t\t\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\tcase \"list\":\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, workspacesEnv{\n\t\t\t\t\tType:       \"workspaces\",\n\t\t\t\t\tWorkspaces: srv.mgr.List(),\n\t\t\t\t})\n\t\t\t}\n\n\t\tcase m := <-incoming:\n\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, msgEnv{\n\t\t\t\tType:      \"message\",\n\t\t\t\tWorkspace: m.Workspace,\n\t\t\t\tMessage:   store.Message(m),\n\t\t\t})\n\n\t\tcase <-tick.C:\n\t\t\t// Push peer count + online list\n\t\t\tif _, _, tracker, ok := srv.mgr.Get(defaultCh); ok {\n\t\t\t\tsrv.writeJSON(conn, srv.presenceEnv(defaultCh, tracker))\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t}\n\t}\n}\n\nfunc (srv *Server) presenceEnv(wsName string, t *presence.Tracker) peersEnv {\n\tonline := t.Online()\n\tids := make([]string, len(online))\n\tfor i, p := range online {\n\t\ts := p.ID.String()\n\t\tif len(s) > 12 {\n\t\t\ts = s[:12]\n\t\t}\n\t\tids[i] = s\n\t}\n\treturn peersEnv{\n\t\tType:      \"peers\",\n\t\tWorkspace: wsName,\n\t\tCount:     len(online),\n\t\tOnline:    ids,\n\t}\n}\n\n// handleUpload receives a file and publishes it.\nfunc (srv *Server) handleUpload(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\tif r.Method != http.MethodPost {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"method not allowed\", http.StatusMethodNotAllowed)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\twsName := r.URL.Query().Get(\"workspace\")\n\tif wsName == \"\" {\n\t\twsName = \"general\"\n\t}\n\tws, s, _, ok := srv.mgr.Get(wsName)\n\tif !ok {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"not joined to workspace: \"+wsName, http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := r.ParseMultipartForm(maxUploadSize); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"file too large (max 10 MB)\", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfile, header, err := r.FormFile(\"file\")\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"missing file field\", http.StatusBadRequest)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tdefer file.Close()\n\n\tdata, err := io.ReadAll(io.LimitReader(file, maxUploadSize+1))\n\tif err != nil || len(data) > maxUploadSize {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"file too large (max 10 MB)\", http.StatusRequestEntityTooLarge)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tfileID := uuid.New().String()\n\tmimeType := header.Header.Get(\"Content-Type\")\n\tif mimeType == \"\" {\n\t\tmimeType = mime.TypeByExtension(filepath.Ext(header.Filename))\n\t}\n\tif mimeType == \"\" {\n\t\tmimeType = \"application/octet-stream\"\n\t}\n\n\tif _, err := s.SaveFile(fileID, header.Filename, data); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"storage error\", http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif err := ws.PublishFile(r.Context(), fileID, header.Filename, mimeType, data); err != nil {\n\t\thttp.Error(w, \"publish error: \"+err.Error(), http.StatusInternalServerError)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n\tjson.NewEncoder(w).Encode(map[string]any{\n\t\t\"ok\": true, \"file_id\": fileID,\n\t\t\"file_name\": header.Filename, \"file_size\": len(data), \"mime\": mimeType,\n\t})\n}\n\n// handleFileDownload serves a saved file. URL: /files/{fileID}/{fileName}\nfunc (srv *Server) handleFileDownload(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\trest := r.URL.Path[len(\"/files/\"):]\n\tslash := len(rest)\n\tfor i, c := range rest {\n\t\tif c == '/' {\n\t\t\tslash = i\n\t\t\tbreak\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\tif slash >= len(rest) {\n\t\thttp.NotFound(w, r)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tfileID   := rest[:slash]\n\tfileName := rest[slash+1:]\n\tfor _, name := range srv.mgr.List() {\n\t\tif _, s, _, ok := srv.mgr.Get(name); ok {\n\t\t\tpath := s.FilePath(fileID, fileName)\n\t\t\tw.Header().Set(\"Content-Disposition\",\n\t\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(`attachment; filename=\"%s\"`, filepath.Base(fileName)))\n\t\t\thttp.ServeFile(w, r, path)\n\t\t\treturn\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\thttp.NotFound(w, r)\n}\n\nfunc (srv *Server) writeJSON(conn *websocket.Conn, v any) {\n\tdata, err := json.Marshal(v)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\t_ = conn.WriteMessage(websocket.TextMessage, data)\n}\n"

  "content": "package main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"path/filepath\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/web\"\n\t\"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/workspace\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"libp2p TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\thttpAddr  := flag.String(\"http\", \":8080\", \"Web UI listen address\")\n\tdefaultCh := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Default channel to join on startup\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Base directory for local stores\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, *port)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tmgr := workspace.New(ps, n.ID, *dataDir)\n\tdefer mgr.CloseAll()\n\n\tif _, _, _, err := mgr.Join(ctx, *defaultCh); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\twebSrv := web.New(n.ID.String(), mgr)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tif err := webSrv.ListenAndServe(ctx, *httpAddr); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[web] %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove-web] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}

18	}

20	// New creates a libp2p host with a fresh Ed25519 identity and listens on
21	// a random TCP port. Call Close() when done.
22	func New(ctx context.Context, listenPort int) (*Node, error) {
23		priv, _, err := crypto.GenerateEd25519Key(rand.Reader)
24		if err != nil {
25			return nil, fmt.Errorf("generate identity: %w", err)
26		}
27	
28		listenAddr := fmt.Sprintf("/ip4/0.0.0.0/tcp/%d", listenPort)
29	
30		h, err := libp2p.New(
31			libp2p.Identity(priv),
32			libp2p.ListenAddrStrings(listenAddr),
33			libp2p.EnableNATService(),
34		)
35		if err != nil {
36			return nil, fmt.Errorf("create host: %w", err)
37		}
38

40			Host: h,

42		}

45		for _, addr := range h.Addrs() {
46			fmt.Printf("[node] listening: %s/p2p/%s\n", addr, n.ID)
47		}
48	
49		return n, nil
50	}
51

55	}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/node/node.go",
  "content": "package node\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\n\tlibp2p \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/crypto\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/multiformats/go-multiaddr\"\n)\n\n// Config controls optional NAT traversal behaviour.\ntype Config struct {\n\t// ListenPort is the TCP port to listen on (0 = random).\n\tListenPort int\n\t// StaticRelays are circuit-relay v2 nodes to use when behind NAT.\n\t// Format: /ip4/<addr>/tcp/<port>/p2p/<peerID>\n\tStaticRelays []string\n}\n\n// Node wraps a libp2p host with GrooveGO-specific state.\ntype Node struct {\n\tHost host.Host\n\tID   peer.ID\n}\n\n// New creates a libp2p host with an Ed25519 identity, NAT traversal, and\n// optional static relay support.\nfunc New(ctx context.Context, cfg Config) (*Node, error) {\n\tpriv, _, err := crypto.GenerateEd25519Key(rand.Reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"generate identity: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tlistenAddr := fmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/tcp/%d\", cfg.ListenPort)\n\n\topts := []libp2p.Option{\n\t\tlibp2p.Identity(priv),\n\t\tlibp2p.ListenAddrStrings(\n\t\t\tlistenAddr,\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/udp/%d/quic-v1\", cfg.ListenPort),\n\t\t),\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableNATService(),  // tell peers our observed public address\n\t\tlibp2p.NATPortMap(),        // UPnP / NAT-PMP port mapping\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableHolePunching(), // DCUtR direct connection upgrade\n\t}\n\n\t// AutoRelay: use supplied static relays or fall back to none.\n\tif len(cfg.StaticRelays) > 0 {\n\t\trelays, err := parseRelays(cfg.StaticRelays)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"parse relay addrs: %w\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t\topts = append(opts, libp2p.EnableAutoRelayWithStaticRelays(relays))\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[node] auto-relay enabled with %d static relay(s)\\n\", len(relays))\n\t}\n\n\th, err := libp2p.New(opts...)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"create host: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tn := &Node{Host: h, ID: h.ID()}\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[node] peer ID : %s\\n\", n.ID)\n\tfor _, addr := range h.Addrs() {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"[node] listening: %s/p2p/%s\\n\", addr, n.ID)\n\t}\n\treturn n, nil\n}\n\n// Close shuts down the libp2p host.\nfunc (n *Node) Close() error {\n\treturn n.Host.Close()\n}\n\nfunc parseRelays(addrs []string) ([]peer.AddrInfo, error) {\n\tout := make([]peer.AddrInfo, 0, len(addrs))\n\tfor _, s := range addrs {\n\t\tma, err := multiaddr.NewMultiaddr(s)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"invalid relay addr %q: %w\", s, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tai, err := peer.AddrInfoFromP2pAddr(ma)\n\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"relay addr info %q: %w\", s, err)\n\t\t}\n\t\tout = append(out, *ai)\n\t}\n\treturn out, nil\n}\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/internal/node/dht.go",
  "content": "package node\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"sync\"\n\n\tdht \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/host\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/peer\"\n\t\"github.com/multiformats/go-multiaddr\"\n)\n\n// DefaultBootstrapPeers are well-known IPFS bootstrap nodes used as DHT\n// entry points when no custom bootstrap peers are provided.\nvar DefaultBootstrapPeers = []string{\n\t\"/dnsaddr/bootstrap.libp2p.io/p2p/REDACTED\",\n\t\"/dnsaddr/bootstrap.libp2p.io/p2p/REDACTED\",\n\t\"/dnsaddr/bootstrap.libp2p.io/p2p/REDACTED\",\n\t\"/dnsaddr/bootstrap.libp2p.io/p2p/REDACTED\",\n}\n\n// StartDHT creates a Kademlia DHT in client mode, bootstraps it, and\n// returns it. The DHT enables WAN peer discovery beyond the local network.\n//\n// bootstrapAddrs overrides DefaultBootstrapPeers when non-empty.\nfunc StartDHT(ctx context.Context, h host.Host, bootstrapAddrs []string) (*dht.IpfsDHT, error) {\n\tif len(bootstrapAddrs) == 0 {\n\t\tbootstrapAddrs = DefaultBootstrapPeers\n\t}\n\n\tkadDHT, err := dht.New(ctx, h, dht.Mode(dht.ModeClient))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"dht create: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\tif err := kadDHT.Bootstrap(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\treturn nil, fmt.Errorf(\"dht bootstrap: %w\", err)\n\t}\n\n\t// Connect to bootstrap peers in parallel; ignore individual failures.\n\tvar wg sync.WaitGroup\n\tconnected := 0\n\tvar mu sync.Mutex\n\n\tfor _, addrStr := range bootstrapAddrs {\n\t\taddrStr := addrStr\n\t\twg.Add(1)\n\t\tgo func() {\n\t\t\tdefer wg.Done()\n\t\t\tma, err := multiaddr.NewMultiaddr(addrStr)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tai, err := peer.AddrInfoFromP2pAddr(ma)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tif err := h.Connect(ctx, *ai); err != nil {\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tmu.Lock()\n\t\t\tconnected++\n\t\t\tmu.Unlock()\n\t\t}()\n\t}\n\twg.Wait()\n\n\tfmt.Printf(\"[dht] bootstrapped (%d/%d peers reached)\\n\", connected, len(bootstrapAddrs))\n\treturn kadDHT, nil\n}\n"
}

  "content": "// groove-relay is a minimal libp2p circuit relay + DHT bootstrap node\n// intended to run on a public VPS. It has no UI — it just keeps running,\n// relaying traffic and helping peers find each other across the internet.\n//\n// Usage:\n//\n//\tgroove-relay --port 4001\n//\n// On startup it prints its full multiaddr. Give that address to groove-web\n// clients via --relay to let them traverse NAT.\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\tlibp2p \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/crypto\"\n\tdht \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/p2p/host/relay\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport := flag.Int(\"port\", 4001, \"TCP listen port\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tpriv, _, err := crypto.GenerateEd25519Key(rand.Reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\th, err := libp2p.New(\n\t\tlibp2p.Identity(priv),\n\t\tlibp2p.ListenAddrStrings(\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/tcp/%d\", *port),\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/udp/%d/quic-v1\", *port),\n\t\t),\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableRelayService(\n\t\t\trelay.WithInfiniteLimits(), // no rate limits for now\n\t\t),\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableNATService(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer h.Close()\n\n\t// Start DHT in server mode so other peers can use us as a bootstrap node.\n\tkadDHT, err := dht.New(ctx, h, dht.Mode(dht.ModeServer))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := kadDHT.Bootstrap(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] node running — share these addresses with your peers:\")\n\tfor _, addr := range h.Addrs() {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"  %s/p2p/%s\\n\", addr, h.ID())\n\t}\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] Ctrl+C to stop\")\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}
```

return nil }\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport      := flag.Int(\"port\", 0, \"libp2p TCP listen port (0 = random)\")\n\thttpAddr  := flag.String(\"http\", \":8080\", \"Web UI listen address\")\n\tdefaultCh := flag.String(\"workspace\", \"general\", \"Default channel to join on startup\")\n\tdataDir   := flag.String(\"data\", defaultDataDir(), \"Base directory for local stores\")\n\tenableDHT := flag.Bool(\"dht\", true, \"Enable Kademlia DHT for WAN peer discovery\")\n\n\tvar relayAddrs  multiFlag\n\tvar bootstrapAddrs multiFlag\n\tflag.Var(&relayAddrs, \"relay\",\n\t\t\"Static relay address (repeat for multiple):\\n\"+\n\t\t\t\"  e.g. /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/<peerID>\")\n\tflag.Var(&bootstrapAddrs, \"bootstrap\",\n\t\t\"DHT bootstrap peer (repeat for multiple; defaults to IPFS bootstrap nodes)\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\t// Boot libp2p node with NAT traversal\n\tn, err := node.New(ctx, node.Config{\n\t\tListenPort:   *port,\n\t\tStaticRelays: []string(relayAddrs),\n\t})\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer n.Close()\n\n\t// mDNS for LAN discovery\n\tmdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer mdnsSvc.Close()\n\n\t// Kademlia DHT for WAN discovery\n\tif *enableDHT {\n\t\tif _, err := node.StartDHT(ctx, n.Host, []string(bootstrapAddrs)); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[dht] warning: %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t\t// non-fatal — LAN still works via mDNS\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tmgr := workspace.New(ps, n.ID, *dataDir)\n\tdefer mgr.CloseAll()\n\n\tif _, _, _, err := mgr.Join(ctx, *defaultCh); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\twebSrv := web.New(n.ID.String(), mgr)\n\tgo func() {\n\t\tif err := webSrv.ListenAndServe(ctx, *httpAddr); err != nil {\n\t\t\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"[web] %s\\n\", err)\n\t\t}\n\t}()\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\tfmt.Println(\"[groove-web] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc defaultDataDir() string {\n\thome, err := os.UserHomeDir()\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\treturn \".groove-data\"\n\t}\n\treturn filepath.Join(home, \".groove\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"bufio"
5		"context"
6		"flag"
7		"fmt"
8		"os"
9		"os/signal"
10		"path/filepath"
11		"strings"
12		"syscall"
13	
14		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node"
15		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store"
16		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport"
17	)
18	
19	func main() {
20		port      := flag.Int("port", 0, "TCP listen port (0 = random)")
21		workspace := flag.String("workspace", "general", "Workspace name to join")
22		dataDir   := flag.String("data", defaultDataDir(), "Directory for local message store")
23		flag.Parse()
24	
25		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
26		defer cancel()
27	
28		// Local persistence
29		s, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(*dataDir, *workspace))
30		if err != nil {
31			fatal(err)
32		}
33		defer s.Close()
34	
35		// Replay history from previous sessions
36		history, err := s.History(*workspace)
37		if err != nil {
38			fatal(err)
39		}
40		if len(history) > 0 {
41			fmt.Printf("[store] replaying %d message(s) from previous session:\n", len(history))
42			for _, m := range history {
43				fmt.Printf("  [%s] %s: %s\n", m.Workspace, shortID(m.From), m.Body)
44			}
45			fmt.Println("[store] --- end of history ---")
46		}
47	
48		// Boot libp2p node
49		n, err := node.New(ctx, *port)
50		if err != nil {
51			fatal(err)
52		}
53		defer n.Close()
54	
55		// mDNS LAN discovery
56		mdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)
57		if err != nil {
58			fatal(err)
59		}
60		defer mdnsSvc.Close()
61	
62		// GossipSub router
63		ps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)
64		if err != nil {
65			fatal(err)
66		}
67	
68		// Join workspace topic (with store for persistence)
69		ws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace, s)
70		if err != nil {
71			fatal(err)
72		}
73	
74		// Receive messages in background
75		go ws.ReadLoop(ctx)
76	
77		fmt.Printf("[groove] workspace: %s — type a message and press Enter\n", *workspace)
78		fmt.Println("[groove] Ctrl+C to quit")
79	
80		quit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
81		signal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
82	
83		scanner := bufio.NewScanner(os.Stdin)
84		input   := make(chan string)
85	
86		go func() {
87			for scanner.Scan() {
88				line := strings.TrimSpace(scanner.Text())
89				if line != "" {
90					input <- line
91				}
92			}
93			close(input)
94		}()
95	
96		for {
97			select {
98			case line, ok := <-input:
99				if !ok {
100					return
101				}
102				if err := ws.Publish(ctx, line); err != nil {
103					fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "[error] publish: %s\n", err)
104				} else {
105					fmt.Printf("[%s] me: %s\n", *workspace, line)
106				}
107			case <-quit:
108				fmt.Println("[groove] shutting down")
109				return
110			}
111		}
112	}
113	
114	func defaultDataDir() string {
115		home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
116		if err != nil {
117			return ".groove-data"
118		}
119		return filepath.Join(home, ".groove")
120	}
121	
122	func shortID(id string) string {
123		if len(id) > 12 {
124			return id[:12]
125		}
126		return id
127	}
128	
129	func fatal(err error) {
130		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "fatal: %s\n", err)
131		os.Exit(1)
132	}
133

1	package main
2	
3	import (
4		"context"
5		"flag"
6		"fmt"
7		"os"
8		"path/filepath"
9	
10		tea "github.com/charmbracelet/bubbletea"
11		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/node"
12		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/store"
13		"github.com/safecast/groove-go/internal/transport"
14	)
15	
16	func main() {
17		port      := flag.Int("port", 0, "TCP listen port (0 = random)")
18		workspace := flag.String("workspace", "general", "Workspace name to join")
19		dataDir   := flag.String("data", defaultDataDir(), "Directory for local message store")
20		flag.Parse()
21	
22		ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
23		defer cancel()
24	
25		// Local persistence
26		s, err := store.Open(filepath.Join(*dataDir, *workspace))
27		if err != nil {
28			fatal(err)
29		}
30		defer s.Close()
31	
32		// Load history to pre-populate the TUI
33		history, err := s.History(*workspace)
34		if err != nil {
35			fatal(err)
36		}
37		histMsgs := make([]incomingMsg, len(history))
38		for i, h := range history {
39			histMsgs[i] = incomingMsg{from: h.From, body: h.Body, workspace: h.Workspace, ts: h.Timestamp}
40		}
41	
42		// Boot libp2p node
43		n, err := node.New(ctx, *port)
44		if err != nil {
45			fatal(err)
46		}
47		defer n.Close()
48	
49		// mDNS LAN discovery
50		mdnsSvc, err := node.StartMDNS(ctx, n.Host)
51		if err != nil {
52			fatal(err)
53		}
54		defer mdnsSvc.Close()
55	
56		// GossipSub router
57		ps, err := transport.NewGossipSub(ctx, n.Host)
58		if err != nil {
59			fatal(err)
60		}
61	
62		// Join workspace topic (with store for persistence)
63		ws, err := transport.JoinWorkspace(ps, n.ID, *workspace, s)
64		if err != nil {
65			fatal(err)
66		}
67	
68		// Bridge pubsub → TUI via buffered channel
69		incoming := make(chan incomingMsg, 64)
70		go ws.ReadLoopInto(ctx, func(m transport.Message) {
71			select {
72			case incoming <- incomingMsg{from: m.From, body: m.Body, workspace: m.Workspace, ts: m.Timestamp}:
73			default:
74			}
75		})
76	
77		getPeers := func() int { return len(ws.ListPeers()) }
78	
79		m := newModel(ctx, *workspace, n.ID.String(), incoming, getPeers, ws.Publish, histMsgs)
80	
81		p := tea.NewProgram(m, tea.WithAltScreen(), tea.WithMouseCellMotion())
82		if _, err := p.Run(); err != nil {
83			fatal(err)
84		}
85	}
86	
87	func defaultDataDir() string {
88		home, err := os.UserHomeDir()
89		if err != nil {
90			return ".groove-data"
91		}
92		return filepath.Join(home, ".groove")
93	}
94	
95	func fatal(err error) {
96		fmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, "fatal: %s\n", err)
97		os.Exit(1)
98	}

{
  "file_path": "/home/rob/Documents/Safecast/GrooveGO/groove-go/cmd/groove-relay/main.go",
  "content": "// groove-relay is a minimal libp2p circuit relay + DHT bootstrap node\n// intended to run on a public VPS. It has no UI — it just keeps running,\n// relaying traffic and helping peers find each other across the internet.\n//\n// Usage:\n//\n//\tgroove-relay --port 4001\n//\n// On startup it prints its full multiaddr. Give that address to groove-web\n// clients via --relay to let them traverse NAT.\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"context\"\n\t\"crypto/rand\"\n\t\"flag\"\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"os\"\n\t\"os/signal\"\n\t\"syscall\"\n\n\tlibp2p \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p\"\n\tdht \"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht\"\n\t\"github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p/core/crypto\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tport := flag.Int(\"port\", 4001, \"TCP listen port\")\n\tflag.Parse()\n\n\tctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())\n\tdefer cancel()\n\n\tpriv, _, err := crypto.GenerateEd25519Key(rand.Reader)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\th, err := libp2p.New(\n\t\tlibp2p.Identity(priv),\n\t\tlibp2p.ListenAddrStrings(\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/tcp/%d\", *port),\n\t\t\tfmt.Sprintf(\"/ip4/0.0.0.0/udp/%d/quic-v1\", *port),\n\t\t),\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableRelayService(), // circuit relay v2\n\t\tlibp2p.EnableNATService(),\n\t)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tdefer h.Close()\n\n\t// Run DHT in server mode — acts as a bootstrap node for WAN discovery.\n\tkadDHT, err := dht.New(ctx, h, dht.Mode(dht.ModeServer))\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\tif err := kadDHT.Bootstrap(ctx); err != nil {\n\t\tfatal(err)\n\t}\n\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] node running — share these addresses with your peers:\")\n\tfor _, addr := range h.Addrs() {\n\t\tfmt.Printf(\"  %s/p2p/%s\\n\", addr, h.ID())\n\t}\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] Ctrl+C to stop\")\n\n\tquit := make(chan os.Signal, 1)\n\tsignal.Notify(quit, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)\n\t<-quit\n\tfmt.Println(\"[relay] shutting down\")\n}\n\nfunc fatal(err error) {\n\tfmt.Fprintf(os.Stderr, \"fatal: %s\\n\", err)\n\tos.Exit(1)\n}\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Pushed `ec3577d`. All 7 phases complete.

**Phase 7 summary:**

| Layer | What it does |
|-------|-------------|
| `EnableHolePunching()` | DCUtR — punches through most home routers directly |
| `NATPortMap()` | UPnP/NAT-PMP — requests a port mapping from the router |
| `EnableAutoRelayWithStaticRelays()` | Falls back through a relay if direct fails |
| Kademlia DHT | Finds peers on the internet, not just LAN |
| `groove-relay` | Deploy this on any VPS to be your own relay/bootstrap node |

**To connect across the internet:**

```bash
# 1. On your VPS — run the relay
groove-relay --port 4001
# prints: /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/QmXxxx...

# 2. On each client — point at the relay
go run ./cmd/groove-web \
  --relay /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/QmXxxx \
  --workspace general
```

---

**All phases done.** GrooveGO now has the full Groove-inspired feature set:

| Phase | Feature |
|-------|---------|
| 1 | P2P node + LAN discovery |
| 2 | GossipSub messaging |
| 3 | Local persistence + history |
| 4 | Binaries for all platforms |
| 5 | Multi-channel workspaces |
| 6 | Presence / online users |
| 7 | NAT traversal + internet peers |

> AGENT

**Option B (pre-built binaries) is the better path** — no Go needed on the VPS or client machines.

## VPS Setup

Build once locally:
```bash
cd groove-go
make linux
```

Copy to your VPS:
```bash
scp dist/groove-relay-linux-amd64 user@1.2.3.4:~/groove-relay
ssh user@1.2.3.4 "chmod +x ~/groove-relay"
```

Run it:
```bash
ssh user@1.2.3.4
./groove-relay --port 4001
```

It prints something like:
```
[relay] node running — share these addresses with your peers:
  /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx...
  /ip4/1.2.3.4/udp/4001/quic-v1/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx...
```

Copy that `/ip4/...` address — clients need it.

---

## Client Setup

Give each client the right binary for their OS from your `dist/` folder:

| OS | Binary |
|----|--------|
| Linux | `groove-web-linux-amd64` |
| Mac Intel | `groove-web-mac-amd64` |
| Mac Apple Silicon | `groove-web-mac-arm64` |
| Windows | `groove-web-windows-amd64.exe` |

Run it:
```bash
./groove-web-linux-amd64 \
  --relay /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx \
  --workspace general \
  --http :8080
```

Then open `http://localhost:8080` in the browser. No Go installation needed.

---

## Keep relay running on VPS

Use `systemd` or simply `screen`/`tmux`:

```bash
# With screen:
screen -S relay
./groove-relay --port 4001
# Ctrl+A D to detach
```

Or a minimal systemd unit:
```ini
[Unit]
Description=Groove Relay

[Service]
ExecStart=/home/user/groove-relay --port 4001
Restart=always

[Install]
WantedBy=multi-user.target
```

```bash
sudo systemctl enable --now groove-relay
```

Also open port 4001 on your VPS firewall (both TCP and UDP for QUIC):
```bash
sudo ufw allow 4001/tcp
sudo ufw allow 4001/udp
```

```
1	# GrooveGO
2	
3	A peer-to-peer collaboration platform in Go, inspired by Microsoft Groove. Decentralized shared workspaces with real-time sync, offline capability, presence awareness, and end-to-end encryption — no central server required.
4	
5	## Vision
6	
7	GrooveGO brings back the best of Groove's architecture on modern P2P primitives:
8	
9	- **Decentralized** — peers connect directly, no central broker
10	- **Offline-first** — CRDT-based sync means you keep working when disconnected
11	- **Encrypted** — Ed25519 identity + per-workspace symmetric keys
12	- **LAN & WAN** — mDNS for local discovery, Kademlia DHT for internet-wide peering
13	
14	## Build Phases
15	
16	| Phase | Status | Feature |
17	|-------|--------|---------|
18	| 1 | ✅ Done | libp2p node bootstrap + mDNS LAN discovery |
19	| 2 | 🔜 Next | GossipSub pubsub — text messaging within workspace topics |
20	| 3 | ⬜ | Persistence — Badger/SQLite local store, replay on reconnect |
21	| 4 | ⬜ | CRDT documents — conflict-free shared state (Automerge) |
22	| 5 | ⬜ | Workspace manager — membership, signed invitations |
23	| 6 | ⬜ | Presence — heartbeats, online/offline status |
24	| 7 | ⬜ | NAT traversal — AutoRelay + hole punching for WAN peers |
25	
26	## Project Structure
27	
28	```
29	groove-go/
30	├── cmd/groove/main.go        # Entry point (--port flag, graceful shutdown)
31	├── internal/
32	│   ├── node/                 # libp2p host, Ed25519 identity, mDNS
33	│   ├── workspace/            # Workspace CRUD, membership (Phase 5)
34	│   ├── sync/                 # CRDT engine, vector clocks (Phase 4)
35	│   ├── store/                # Local persistence (Phase 3)
36	│   ├── transport/            # GossipSub, direct messaging (Phase 2)
37	│   └── presence/             # Online/offline tracking (Phase 6)
38	└── pkg/protocol/             # Protobuf message definitions
39	```
40	
41	## Getting Started
42	
43	**Requirements:** Go 1.22+
44	
45	```bash
46	git clone https://github.com/robouden/GrooveGO.git
47	cd GrooveGO/groove-go
48	go mod download
49	```
50	
51	**Run two nodes on the same LAN** — they will discover each other automatically via mDNS:
52	
53	```bash
54	# Terminal 1
55	go run ./cmd/groove --port 9000
56	
57	# Terminal 2
58	go run ./cmd/groove --port 9001
59	```
60	
61	Each node prints its peer ID and listen address on startup. Once discovered, peers connect automatically.
62	
63	## Architecture
64	
65	### Phase 1 — Node Boot & LAN Discovery
66	
67	```mermaid
68	sequenceDiagram
69	    participant A as Peer A (groove node)
70	    participant M as mDNS (LAN multicast)
71	    participant B as Peer B (groove node)
72	
73	    A->>A: Generate Ed25519 keypair
74	    A->>A: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)
75	    A->>M: Advertise service tag "groove-go.local"
76	
77	    B->>B: Generate Ed25519 keypair
78	    B->>B: Start libp2p host (TCP listen)
79	    B->>M: Advertise service tag "groove-go.local"
80	
81	    M-->>A: Notify: Peer B found (AddrInfo)
82	    M-->>B: Notify: Peer A found (AddrInfo)
83	
84	    A->>B: libp2p Connect (Noise/TLS handshake)
85	    B-->>A: Connected ✓
86	```
87	
88	### Full System — Component Interaction (All Phases)
89	
90	```mermaid
91	flowchart TD
92	    subgraph Node["node (Phase 1)"]
93	        ID["Ed25519 Identity\n(Peer ID = pubkey hash)"]
94	        HOST["libp2p Host\n(TCP / QUIC / WebRTC)"]
95	        MDNS["mDNS Discovery\n(LAN)"]
96	        DHT["Kademlia DHT\n(WAN)"]
97	        RELAY["AutoRelay / Hole Punch\n(NAT Traversal)"]
98	        ID --> HOST
99	        HOST --> MDNS
100	        HOST --> DHT
101	        HOST --> RELAY
102	    end
103	
104	    subgraph Transport["transport (Phase 2)"]
105	        PS["GossipSub PubSub"]
106	        DM["Direct Stream Messages"]
107	    end
108	
109	    subgraph Store["store (Phase 3)"]
110	        DB["Badger / SQLite\n(local persistence)"]
111	        VCL["Vector Clocks\n(sync state)"]
112	    end
113	
114	    subgraph Sync["sync (Phase 4)"]
115	        CRDT["Automerge CRDT\nDocuments"]
116	        MERGE["Conflict-Free Merge\non Reconnect"]
117	        CRDT --> MERGE
118	    end
119	
120	    subgraph Workspace["workspace (Phase 5)"]
121	        WS["Workspace Manager\n(create / join)"]
122	        MEM["Membership List\n(signed invitations)"]
123	        SYMKEY["Symmetric Key\n(per workspace)"]
124	        WS --> MEM
125	        WS --> SYMKEY
126	    end
127	
128	    subgraph Presence["presence (Phase 6)"]
129	        HB["Heartbeat\n(periodic pubsub ping)"]
130	        STATUS["Online / Offline\nStatus Map"]
131	        HB --> STATUS
132	    end
133	
134	    HOST --> PS
135	    HOST --> DM
136	    PS --> DB
137	    DM --> DB
138	    DB --> VCL
139	    VCL --> MERGE
140	    PS --> WS
141	    PS --> HB
142	    WS --> CRDT
143	```
144	
145	### Data Sync — Offline & Reconnect Flow
146	
147	```mermaid
148	sequenceDiagram
149	    participant A as Peer A (online)
150	    participant DB_A as Store A
151	    participant DB_B as Store B
152	    participant B as Peer B (was offline)
153	
154	    Note over B: Goes offline
155	    A->>DB_A: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)
156	    B->>DB_B: Write CRDT ops (vector clock advances)
157	
158	    Note over B: Comes back online
159	    B->>A: Connect (mDNS / DHT)
160	    A->>B: Exchange vector clocks
161	    B->>A: Send missing ops (delta sync)
162	    A->>B: Send missing ops (delta sync)
163	    A->>A: Merge (conflict-free)
164	    B->>B: Merge (conflict-free)
165	    Note over A,B: Consistent state ✓
166	```
167	
168	Full standalone diagram files are in [diagrams/](diagrams/).
169	
170	## Key Libraries
171	
172	| Purpose | Library |
173	|---------|---------|
174	| P2P networking | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p` |
175	| Pub/Sub | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-pubsub` |
176	| DHT discovery | `github.com/libp2p/go-libp2p-kad-dht` |
177	| Serialization | `google.golang.org/protobuf` |
178	| Local DB | `github.com/dgraph-io/badger` |
179	| CRDTs | `github.com/automerge/automerge-go` |
180	
181	## Connection to Safecast
182	
183	The CRDT + offline-first design maps naturally onto distributed sensor networks. bGeigieZen devices could form mesh workspaces, syncing measurement data peer-to-peer without the central API, then reconciling with the server when connectivity allows.
184

Once discovered, peers connect automatically.",
  "new_string": "## Project Structure\n\n```\ngroove-go/\n├── cmd/\n│   ├── groove/           # Headless CLI node\n│   ├── groove-ui/        # Bubble Tea terminal UI\n│   ├── groove-web/       # Browser UI + WebSocket server\n│   └── groove-relay/     # VPS relay/bootstrap node\n├── internal/\n│   ├── node/             # libp2p host, Ed25519 identity, mDNS, DHT\n│   ├── workspace/        # Multi-channel workspace manager\n│   ├── store/            # Badger persistence + file storage\n│   ├── transport/        # GossipSub pubsub, file sharing\n│   ├── presence/         # Heartbeat tracker, online/offline status\n│   └── web/              # HTTP server, WebSocket bridge, embedded UI\n└── Makefile              # Cross-platform build targets\n```\n\n## Getting Started\n\n### Option A — Pre-built binaries (no Go required)\n\nDownload the appropriate binary for your OS from the [Releases](https://github.com/robouden/GrooveGO/releases) page, or build all platforms at once:\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/robouden/GrooveGO.git\ncd GrooveGO/groove-go\nmake all          # builds linux/mac/windows into dist/\n```\n\n### Option B — Run from source\n\n**Requirements:** Go 1.22+\n\n```bash\ngit clone https://github.com/robouden/GrooveGO.git\ncd GrooveGO/groove-go\ngo run ./cmd/groove-web --workspace general\n```\n\n---\n\n## Deployment\n\n### 1. Run a relay node on a VPS\n\nThe relay helps clients behind NAT find and connect to each other across the internet.\n\n```bash\n# Copy binary to VPS (or build from source)\nscp dist/groove-relay-linux-amd64 user@YOUR_VPS_IP:~/groove-relay\nssh user@YOUR_VPS_IP \"chmod +x ~/groove-relay\"\n\n# Open firewall ports (TCP + UDP for QUIC)\nsudo ufw allow 4001/tcp\nsudo ufw allow 4001/udp\n\n# Start the relay\n./groove-relay --port 4001\n```\n\nOn startup it prints its full multiaddr — copy it, clients need it:\n\n```\n[relay] node running — share these addresses with your peers:\n  /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx...\n  /ip4/1.2.3.4/udp/4001/quic-v1/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx...\n```\n\n**Keep it running with systemd:**\n\n```ini\n# /etc/systemd/system/groove-relay.service\n[Unit]\nDescription=Groove Relay Node\n\n[Service]\nExecStart=/home/user/groove-relay --port 4001\nRestart=always\n\n[Install]\nWantedBy=multi-user.target\n```\n\n```bash\nsudo systemctl enable --now groove-relay\n```\n\n### 2. Connect clients via the relay\n\nGive each client the binary for their platform and the relay address:\n\n```bash\n# Linux\n./groove-web-linux-amd64 \\\n  --relay /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx \\\n  --workspace general \\\n  --http :8080\n\n# Mac\n./groove-web-mac-arm64 \\\n  --relay /ip4/1.2.3.4/tcp/4001/p2p/12D3KooWXxxx \\\n  --workspace general \\\n  --http :8080\n```\n\nOpen `http://localhost:8080` in the browser. No Go installation needed on client machines.\n\n| Platform | Binary |\n|----------|--------|\n| Linux x64 | `groove-web-linux-amd64` |\n| Linux ARM64 | `groove-web-linux-arm64` |\n| Mac Intel | `groove-web-mac-amd64` |\n| Mac Apple Silicon | `groove-web-mac-arm64` |\n| Windows | `groove-web-windows-amd64.exe` |\n\n### LAN-only (no relay needed)\n\nOn the same local network, peers discover each other automatically via mDNS — no relay or port forwarding required:\n\n```bash\n# Just run on each machine, no --relay flag needed\n./groove-web-linux-amd64 --workspace general --http :8080\n```"
}
